<script>
/* ===================== Page logic (no need to edit) ===================== */
const PUBLIC_BUILD = false;  // editable source; Download PUBLIC creates the visitor-only copy
const DRAFT_KEY = "ppz_draft", UNLOCK_KEY = "ppz_unlock_until", SESSION_HOURS = 10;
const $ = id => document.getElementById(id);
const esc = s => String(s ?? "").replace(/[&<>"']/g, c => ({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
const bold = s => esc(s).replace(/\*\*(.+?)\*\*/g, "<b>$1</b>");
const STATUS = {
  working:     { cls: "working", label: "working!" },
  unknown:     { cls: "unknown", label: "???" },
  unknownlink: { cls: "unknown", label: "Unknown Link?" },
  blocked:     { cls: "blocked", label: "blocked" }
};
const st = s => STATUS[s] || STATUS.unknown;

// Load draft from this device if one exists
const BUILD = "2026-11-11-a";   // bump this whenever the page code/data is replaced with a new version
const normalize = d => {
  d._build = BUILD;
  d.canvas = d.canvas || { height: 520, items: [] };
  d.textPanels = Array.isArray(d.textPanels) ? d.textPanels : [];
  d.textPanels.forEach((p, i) => {
    p.title ??= "Text panel"; p.text ??= ""; p.image ??= "";
    p.uploadKey ??= "tp_image_" + (p.id || i);
    p.imageFit ??= "cover";
    p.wide ??= false; p.glow ??= false; p.collapsible ??= false;
    p.startCollapsed ??= false; p.copy ??= true; p.draft ??= false;
  });
  return d;
};
const EXPORTED_DATA = normalize(JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(DATA)));   // what this file shipped with
let D = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(DATA)), hasDraft = false;
try {
  const saved = PUBLIC_BUILD ? null : localStorage.getItem(DRAFT_KEY);
  if (saved) {
    const d = JSON.parse(saved);
    if (d._build === BUILD) { D = d; hasDraft = true; }
    else localStorage.removeItem(DRAFT_KEY);   // leftover draft from an older version: ignore it
  }
} catch (e) {}
normalize(D);
let undoStack = [];
let lastState = JSON.stringify(D);
let lastUndoAt = 0;

const img = key => (D.images || {})[key] || "";

/* ---------- Public page ---------- */
let pvIdx = -1; // which usable link the preview is showing
let tileAnim = true; // cards slide in only on the first draw, not on every search keystroke
function tile(p, i) {
  const src = img(p.image);
  const pic = src
    ? `<img class="pic ${p.imageFit === "contain" ? "image-fit" : ""}" src="${esc(src)}" alt="${esc(p.name)}" loading="lazy">`
    : `<div class="ph">${esc((p.name || "?").charAt(0))}</div>`;
  const dots = (p.links || []).map(l => `<i class="dot ${st(l.status).cls}"></i>`).join("");
  const enter = tileAnim ? ` enter" style="animation-delay:${Math.min(i, 12) * 70}ms` : "";
  return `<div class="tile${enter}" tabindex="0" data-i="${i}">${pic}
    <div class="info"><h3>${esc(p.name)}</h3><small>click to see more</small>
    <div class="mini">${dots}</div></div></div>`;
}
function renderTop() {
  const list = D.proxies.map((p, i) => [p, i]).filter(([p]) => p.top).slice(0, 5);
  $("top5").innerHTML = list.map(([p, i]) => tile(p, i)).join("") || `<div class="empty">No top picks yet.</div>`;
}
function renderAll() {
  const q = $("search").value.toLowerCase().trim();
  const list = D.proxies.map((p, i) => [p, i]).filter(([p]) =>
    [p.name, p.aka, p.note, ...(p.tags || [])].join(" ").toLowerCase().includes(q));
  $("all").innerHTML = list.map(([p, i]) => tile(p, i)).join("") || `<div class="empty">No proxies match.</div>`;
}
function textPanelCard(p, i, preview = false) {
  const classes = ["tp-card", p.wide ? "wide" : "", p.glow ? "glow" : ""].filter(Boolean).join(" ");
  const title = esc(p.title || "Untitled panel");
  const src = img(p.image);
  const picture = src ? `<img class="tp-picture ${p.imageFit === "contain" ? "fit" : ""}" src="${esc(src)}" alt="${title}" loading="lazy">` : "";
  const draft = p.draft && !PUBLIC_BUILD ? `<span class="tp-draft">DRAFT · visitors won't see this</span>` : "";
  const copy = p.copy !== false ? `<button type="button" class="btn sm tp-copy" data-act="copyTextPanel" data-i="${i}">📋 Copy text</button>` : "";
  const content = `<div class="tp-content">${picture}<div class="tp-text">${bold(p.text || "")}</div>${copy}</div>`;
  if (p.collapsible && !preview) {
    return `<details class="${classes}" ${p.startCollapsed ? "" : "open"}><summary>${title}${draft}</summary>${content}</details>`;
  }
  return `<article class="${classes}"><h3>${title}${draft}</h3>${content}</article>`;
}

function renderTextPanels() {
  const list = (D.textPanels || []).map((p, i) => ({ p, i })).filter(({ p }) => !p.draft || !PUBLIC_BUILD);
  const box = $("textPanelsBox"), target = $("textPanels");
  if (!box || !target) return;
  box.hidden = list.length === 0;
  target.innerHTML = list.map(({ p, i }) => textPanelCard(p, i)).join("");
}

function renderPage() {
  const S = D.site;
  $("siteTitle").textContent = S.title;
  $("siteTagline").textContent = S.tagline;
  $("siteVersion").textContent = S.version;
  $("scrollHint").textContent = S.scrollHint;
  $("topText").textContent = S.topText;
  $("tips").textContent = S.tips;
  $("footer").textContent = S.footer;
  document.title = S.title;
  $("updatesBox").hidden = !D.updates.length;
  $("updates").innerHTML = D.updates.map(u => `<li>${bold(u)}</li>`).join("");
  $("rules").innerHTML = D.rules.map(r => `<li>${bold(r)}</li>`).join("");
  $("soonBox").hidden = !D.comingSoon.items.length;
  $("soonText").textContent = D.comingSoon.text;
  $("soonItems").innerHTML = D.comingSoon.items.map(i => `<span class="tag">${esc(i)}</span>`).join("");
  renderTextPanels();
  renderTop(); renderAll();
}
let renderTimer;
const queueRender = () => { clearTimeout(renderTimer); renderTimer = setTimeout(renderPage, 250); };

function openProxy(i) {
  const p = D.proxies[i]; if (!p) return;
  pvIdx = -1;
  const src = img(p.image);
  $("sheet").innerHTML = `
    <button class="close" aria-label="Close">×</button>
    ${src ? `<img class="${p.imageFit === "contain" ? "image-fit" : ""}" src="${esc(src)}" alt="${esc(p.name)}">` : ""}
    <h2>${esc(p.name)}</h2>
    ${p.aka ? `<div class="aka">${esc(p.aka)}</div>` : ""}
    <div class="note">${esc(p.note)}</div>
    <div class="tags">${(p.tags || []).map(t => `<span class="tag">${esc(t)}</span>`).join("")}</div>
    ${p.links?.length ? `
      <button class="btn preview-btn" data-act="previewSite" data-i="${i}">◀ Preview site</button>
      <div class="preview-box" id="previewBox"></div>
    ` : ""}
    <div class="links">${(p.links || []).map((l, n) => {
      const s = st(l.status);
      return `<a class="link ${s.cls}" href="${esc(l.url)}" target="_blank" rel="noopener">
        <i class="dot"></i>Link ${n + 1}<span class="st">${s.label}</span></a>`;
    }).join("")}</div>`;
  $("modal").classList.add("open");
}
const closeModal = () => $("modal").classList.remove("open");

/* ---------- Developer panel ---------- */
const hash = (str, seed = 7) => {
  let h1 = 0xdeadbeef ^ seed, h2 = 0x41c6ce57 ^ seed;
  for (let i = 0, ch; i < str.length; i++) {
    ch = str.charCodeAt(i);
    h1 = Math.imul(h1 ^ ch, 2654435761);
    h2 = Math.imul(h2 ^ ch, 1597334677);
  }
  h1 = Math.imul(h1 ^ (h1 >>> 16), 2246822507) ^ Math.imul(h2 ^ (h2 >>> 13), 3266489909);
  h2 = Math.imul(h2 ^ (h2 >>> 16), 2246822507) ^ Math.imul(h1 ^ (h1 >>> 13), 3266489909);
  return (4294967296 * (2097151 & h2) + (h1 >>> 0)).toString(36);
};
async function hashStr(s) {
  const salted = "ppz|" + s;
  if (window.crypto && crypto.subtle) {
    const buf = await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", new TextEncoder().encode(salted));
    return "s:" + [...new Uint8Array(buf)].map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join("");
  }
  return "h:" + hash(salted);
}
const getPath = (o, p) => p.split(".").reduce((a, k) => a?.[k], o);
const setPath = (o, p, v) => {
  const k = p.split("."); let t = o;
  for (let i = 0; i < k.length - 1; i++) t = t[k[i]];
  t[k[k.length - 1]] = v;
};
const isUnlocked = () => +localStorage.getItem(UNLOCK_KEY) > Date.now();
const unlock = () => localStorage.setItem(UNLOCK_KEY, Date.now() + SESSION_HOURS * 3600000);

let tab = "proxies";
const openSet = new Set();
const textPanelOpenSet = new Set();

function toast(msg) {
  const t = $("toast"); t.textContent = msg; t.classList.add("show");
  clearTimeout(t._h); t._h = setTimeout(() => t.classList.remove("show"), 2200);
}
function save() {
  const current = JSON.stringify(D);
  if (current !== lastState) {
    // Group rapid typing into one undo step.
    if (Date.now() - lastUndoAt > 700) {
      undoStack.push(lastState);
      if (undoStack.length > 50) undoStack.shift();
      lastUndoAt = Date.now();
    }
    lastState = current;
  }
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(DRAFT_KEY, current);
    hasDraft = true;
  } catch (e) {
    toast("⚠ Could not save on this device. Download your file.");
  }
  queueRender();
}
function updateFab() {
  if (PUBLIC_BUILD) return;
  const fab = $("fab");
  if (!isUnlocked()) { fab.hidden = true; return; }
  const ms = +localStorage.getItem(UNLOCK_KEY) - Date.now();
  const h = Math.floor(ms / 3600000), m = Math.floor((ms % 3600000) / 60000);
  fab.textContent = `⚙ DEV · ${h}h ${m}m`;
  fab.hidden = $("dev").classList.contains("open");
}
setInterval(updateFab, 30000);

function tryOpenDev() {
  if (PUBLIC_BUILD) return;
  if (isUnlocked()) { openDev(); return; }
  const first = !D.devHash;
  $("sheet").innerHTML = `
    <button class="close" aria-label="Close">×</button>
    <h2>${first ? "🔧 Set up login" : "🔒 Developer login"}</h2>
    <p class="sub">${first ? "Choose your username and password. You only do this once." : "Enter your username and password."}</p>
    <input id="userIn" placeholder="Username" autocapitalize="off" autocorrect="off" autocomplete="username" style="margin-bottom:10px">
    <input type="password" id="passIn" placeholder="Password" autocomplete="current-password" style="margin-bottom:8px">
    <label class="check"><input type="checkbox" onchange="$('passIn').type = this.checked ? 'text' : 'password'"> Show password</label>
    <button class="btn pri full" data-act="checkPass">${first ? "Save &amp; unlock" : "Unlock"}</button>`;
  $("modal").classList.add("open");
  setTimeout(() => $("userIn")?.focus(), 50);
}
function openDev() {
  closeModal();
  $("dev").classList.add("open");
  document.body.style.overflow = "hidden";
  renderDev(); updateFab();
}
function closeDev() {
  $("dev").classList.remove("open");
  document.body.style.overflow = "";
  if (location.hash === "#dev") history.replaceState(null, "", location.pathname + location.search);
  updateFab();
}

const opts = (list, cur) => list.map(([v, label]) =>
  `<option value="${esc(v)}" ${v === cur ? "selected" : ""}>${esc(label)}</option>`).join("");
const field = (label, path, val, area) => `<label class="field"><span>${label}</span>${
  area ? `<textarea data-path="${path}">${esc(val)}</textarea>`
       : `<input data-path="${path}" value="${esc(val)}">`}</label>`;

const TABS = {

      </details>`).join("");
  },
  text() {
    const S = "site";
    const imageOpts = [["", "(no picture)"], ...Object.keys(D.images || {}).map(k => [k, k])];
    const panels = (D.textPanels || []).map((p, i) => `

          ${v ? `<img class="thumb" src="${esc(v)}" alt="">` : `<div class="thumb">none</div>`}
          <div class="meta"><b>${esc(k)}</b>
            <small>${!v ? "empty" : v.startsWith("data:") ? `uploaded · ~${kb} KB` : "web link"}</small></div>
          <div class="row" style="width:100%;margin:0">
            <span class="btn sm upl">Upload<input type="file" accept="image/*" data-key="${esc(k)}"></span>
            <button class="btn sm" data-act="linkImage" data-key="${esc(k)}">Use link</button>
            <button class="btn sm danger" data-act="delImage" data-key="${esc(k)}" style="margin-left:auto">Delete</button>
          </div>
        </div>`;
      }).join("");
  },
  save() {
    const kb = Math.round(buildHTML().length / 1024);
    const dirty = JSON.stringify(D) !== JSON.stringify(EXPORTED_DATA);
    return (dirty
      ? `<div class="warn">🟡 <b>Unpublished edits.</b> They are saved on this device only. Your live site does not change until you download the updated file and upload it to your site.</div>`
      : `<div class="warn" style="color:var(--good);border-color:var(--good);background:rgba(61,220,132,.1)">🟢 <b>Up to date.</b> This matches the file you loaded. Edits save on this device automatically; to publish new ones, download the file and upload it.</div>`) +
      `<p class="sub">Updated file size: about <b>${kb} KB</b> · Page version: <b>${BUILD}</b></p>
      <button class="btn full" data-act="undo">↶ Undo last change</button>
      <button class="btn pri full" data-act="downloadPublic">⬇ Download PUBLIC version (upload this to your site)</button>
      <p class="sub">The public copy has no developer panel and no stored login, so visitors can't open it.</p>
      <button class="btn full" data-act="download">⬇ Download editable copy (keep this for yourself)</button>
      <button class="btn full" data-act="copyHTML">📋 Copy full code instead</button>
      <h3>🔒 Login</h3>
      <p class="sub">${D.devHash ? "A login is set. Fill these in only if you want to change it." : "No login set yet."}</p>
      <input id="newUser" placeholder="New username" autocapitalize="off" autocorrect="off" style="margin-bottom:8px">
      <input type="password" id="newPass" placeholder="New password" style="margin-bottom:8px">
      <button class="btn full" data-act="setPass">Save new login</button>
      <button class="btn full" data-act="lock">Lock now</button>
      <h3>⚠ Danger zone</h3>
      <button class="btn full danger" data-act="discard">Discard device edits</button>`;
  }
};

function renderDev() {
  [...$("devTabs").children].forEach(b => b.classList.toggle("on", b.dataset.tab === tab));
  $("devBody").innerHTML = TABS[tab]();
}

function buildHTML(pub = false) {
  const c = document.documentElement.cloneNode(true);
  {  // remove leftovers from the Claude preview or browser extensions, but NEVER the main app, the data or an add-on
    const EXT = ["chrome-" + "extension://", "moz-" + "extension://"];
    const PRE = ["__cowork" + "_html_review_ready__", "__cowork" + "HtmlReviewGuest", "real" + "Parent", "claude-" + "scrollbar", "__cwhr" + "-sel"];
    const hit = (t, list) => list.some(m => t.includes(m));
    c.querySelectorAll("style").forEach(x => { const t = x.textContent; if (hit(t, EXT) || hit(t, PRE)) x.remove(); });
    c.querySelectorAll("script").forEach(x => {
      const t = x.textContent;
      if (x.id || t.includes("const PUBLIC" + "_BUILD")) return;
      if (hit(t, EXT) || hit(t, PRE)) x.remove();
    });
  }
  c.querySelectorAll("[data-dyn]").forEach(e => { e.innerHTML = ""; e.className = e.className.replace(/\bshow\b/, "").trim(); });
  c.querySelector("#modal").classList.remove("open");
  c.querySelector("#dev").classList.remove("open");
  c.querySelector("#fab").hidden = true;
  c.querySelector("body").classList.remove("editing");
  c.querySelector("#edit").hidden = true;
  {  // opened inside the Claude preview, <head> items land inside <body>: put them back, drop extension junk
    const hd = c.querySelector("head"), bd = c.querySelector("body");
    if (hd && bd) {
      [...bd.children].forEach(n => { if (/^(META|TITLE|LINK)$/.test(n.tagName) || (n.tagName === "STYLE" && !n.id)) hd.appendChild(n); });
      if (bd.id === "artifacts-component-root-html") bd.removeAttribute("id");
      c.querySelectorAll("plasmo-csui, [id$='extension-root']").forEach(x => x.remove());
      c.classList.remove("ppz-bgon"); if (!c.getAttribute("class")) c.removeAttribute("class");
      if (!bd.getAttribute("class")) bd.removeAttribute("class");
    }
  }
  c.querySelector("body").removeAttribute("style");
  const out = pub ? { ...D, devHash: "" } : D;          // public copy never carries the login
  const json = JSON.stringify(out, null, 2).replace(/<\/script/gi, "<\\/script");
  c.querySelector("#settings").textContent = `\nconst DATA = ${json};\n`;
  if (pub) {
    c.querySelector("#dev").replaceChildren();           // developer panel removed
    c.querySelector("#fab").remove();
    c.querySelectorAll("script").forEach(sc => {
      if (sc.id !== "settings") sc.textContent = sc.textContent.replace(/^const PUBLIC_BUILD = false;/m, "const PUBLIC_BUILD = true;");
    });
  }
  return "<!DOCTYPE html>\n" + c.outerHTML;
}

const ACT = {
  openDev: tryOpenDev,
  downloadPublic() {
    const blob = new Blob([buildHTML(true)], { type: "text/html" });
    const a = document.createElement("a");
    a.href = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    a.download = "ProxyPathz-public.html";
    document.body.appendChild(a); a.click(); a.remove();
    setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(a.href), 5000);
    toast("Downloading public version…");
  },
  gAdd(el) {
    if (G.length >= GMAX) return toast("Limit reached: " + GMAX + " items");
    const t = el.dataset.t, tx = t === "text";
    const it = cleanItem({ type: t, x: 30, y: 30, w: tx ? 40 : 25, h: tx ? 12 : 18, text: tx ? "Hello" : "", fill: gNew, color: "#ffffff", size: 18 });
    G.push(it); gSel = it.id; gSave(); renderGuest();
  },
  gDel() { G = G.filter(i => i.id !== gSel); gSel = null; gSave(); renderGuest(); },
  gClear() { if (!confirm("Clear your whole guest canvas?")) return; G = []; gSel = null; gSave(); renderGuest(); },
  gCopy() {
    navigator.clipboard.writeText(JSON.stringify({ ppz: 1, items: G.map(({ id, ...r }) => r) }))
      .then(() => toast("Copied! Send this code to the owner.")).catch(() => toast("Copy failed"));
  },
  importGuest() {
    let data; try { data = JSON.parse($("gImport").value); } catch { return toast("That isn't a valid guest code"); }
    const items = (Array.isArray(data?.items) ? data.items : []).slice(0, GMAX).map(cleanItem).filter(Boolean);
    if (!items.length) return toast("No usable items in that code");
    D.canvas.items.push(...items); save(); renderCanvas(); renderDev();
    toast("Added " + items.length + " item(s) to your canvas");
  },
  undo() {
    if (!undoStack.length) return toast("Nothing to undo yet");
    D = JSON.parse(undoStack.pop());
    lastState = JSON.stringify(D);
    try { localStorage.setItem(DRAFT_KEY, lastState); hasDraft = true; } catch (e) {}
    if (!sel()) selId = null;
    renderPage(); renderDev(); renderEd();
    toast("Undid the last change");
  },
  discard() {
    if (!confirm("Discard device-saved edits and restore this file's original settings?")) return;
    D = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(EXPORTED_DATA));
    undoStack = [];
    lastState = JSON.stringify(D);
    localStorage.removeItem(DRAFT_KEY);
    hasDraft = false;
    selId = null;
    renderPage(); renderDev(); renderEd();
    toast("Device edits discarded");
  },
  editCanvas() { closeDev(); document.body.classList.add("editing"); renderCanvas(); renderEd(); $("canvasBox").scrollIntoView({ behavior: "smooth" }); },
  edDone() { document.body.classList.remove("editing"); selId = null; renderCanvas(); renderEd(); openDev(); },
  edAdd(el) {
    const t = el.dataset.t, tx = t === "text";
    const it = { id: "e" + Date.now().toString(36), type: t, x: 30, y: 30, w: tx ? 40 : 25, h: tx ? 12 : 18,
      text: tx ? "New text" : t === "button" ? "Button" : "", size: 18, color: "#ffffff", fill: newColor,
      fillA: tx ? 0 : 100, op: 0, radius: t === "button" ? 10 : 0, image: "", font: "Default", link: "" };
    D.canvas.items.push(it); selId = it.id; save(); renderCanvas(); renderEd();
  },
  edFwd() { edMove(1); },
  edBack() { edMove(-1); },
  edDel() {
    if (!confirm("Delete this item?")) return;
    D.canvas.items = D.canvas.items.filter(i => i.id !== selId); selId = null; save(); renderCanvas(); renderEd();
  },
  toggleFit(el) {
    const p = D.proxies[+el.dataset.i];
    p.imageFit = p.imageFit === "contain" ? "cover" : "contain";
    save();
    renderDev();
  },
  previewSite(el) {
    const p = D.proxies[+el.dataset.i];
    const box = $("previewBox");
    if (!p || !box) return;
    // Only real web links that aren't marked blocked
    const usable = [];
    (p.links || []).forEach((l, n) => {
      if (l.status === "blocked") return;
      try {
        const u = new URL(l.url);
        if (["https:", "http:"].includes(u.protocol)) usable.push({ n, href: u.href });
      } catch {}
    });
    if (!usable.length) return toast("No usable links to preview");

    pvIdx = (pvIdx + 1) % usable.length;   // every tap moves to the next link
    const cur = usable[pvIdx];

    const cap = document.createElement("div");
    cap.className = "preview-cap";
    cap.textContent = `Showing link ${cur.n + 1} (${pvIdx + 1} of ${usable.length})` +
      (usable.length > 1 ? " · blank? tap again for the next one" : "");

    const frame = document.createElement("iframe");
    frame.src = cur.href;
    frame.title = `Preview of ${p.name}, link ${cur.n + 1}`;
    frame.referrerPolicy = "no-referrer";
    frame.setAttribute("sandbox", "allow-scripts allow-forms");

    const hide = document.createElement("button");
    hide.className = "btn sm";
    hide.dataset.act = "hidePreview";
    hide.textContent = "Hide preview";

    box.replaceChildren(cap, frame, hide);
    box.classList.add("open");
    el.textContent = usable.length > 1 ? "↻ Try next link" : "↻ Reload";
  },
  hidePreview() {
    const box = $("previewBox");
    if (!box) return;
    box.classList.remove("open");
    box.replaceChildren();
    pvIdx = -1;
    const b = document.querySelector(".preview-btn");
    if (b) b.textContent = "◀ Preview site";
  },
  closeDev,
  tab: el => { tab = el.dataset.tab; renderDev(); $("devBody").scrollTop = 0; },
  async checkPass() {
    const u = $("userIn").value.trim(), p = $("passIn").value;
    if (!u || !p) return toast("Enter both username and password");
    const h = await hashStr(u + "\n" + p);
    if (!D.devHash) {
      D.devHash = h; save(); unlock(); openDev();
      toast("Login saved. Download the file to keep it.");
    } else if (h === D.devHash) {
      unlock(); openDev();
    } else {
      toast("Wrong username or password");
    }
  },
  addProxy() {
    D.proxies.push({ name: "New proxy", aka: "", image: "", top: false, note: "", tags: [],
      links: [{ url: "https://", status: "unknown" }] });
    openSet.add(D.proxies.length - 1); save(); renderDev();
  },
  addTextPanel() {
    const id = "tp" + Date.now().toString(36);
    const index = D.textPanels.length;
    D.textPanels.push({ id, title: "New text panel", text: "Write your message here.", image: "",
      uploadKey: "tp_image_" + id, imageFit: "cover", wide: false, glow: false,
      collapsible: false, startCollapsed: false, copy: true, draft: false });
    textPanelOpenSet.clear(); textPanelOpenSet.add(index);
    save(); renderDev();
  },
  delTextPanel(el) {
    const i = +el.dataset.i, p = D.textPanels[i];
    if (!p) return;
    if (!confirm(`Delete text panel "${p.title || "Untitled panel"}"?`)) return;
    if (p.uploadKey) delete D.images[p.uploadKey];
    D.textPanels.splice(i, 1);
    textPanelOpenSet.clear();
    save(); renderDev();
  },
  async copyTextPanel(el) {
    const p = D.textPanels[+el.dataset.i];
    if (!p) return;
    const value = String(p.text || "");
    if (!value.trim()) return toast("There is no text to copy");
    try {
      if (navigator.clipboard && window.isSecureContext) {
        await navigator.clipboard.writeText(value);
      } else {
        const ta = document.createElement("textarea");
        ta.value = value; ta.style.position = "fixed"; ta.style.opacity = "0";
        document.body.appendChild(ta); ta.select();
        const ok = document.execCommand("copy"); ta.remove();
        if (!ok) throw new Error("copy failed");
      }
      toast("Text copied");
    } catch (e) { toast("Copy failed"); }
  },
  delProxy(el) {
    const i = +el.dataset.i;
    if (!confirm(`Delete "${D.proxies[i].name}"?`)) return;
    D.proxies.splice(i, 1); openSet.clear(); save(); renderDev();
  },
  moveProxy(el) {
    const i = +el.dataset.i, j = i + +el.dataset.d;
    if (j < 0 || j >= D.proxies.length) return;
    [D.proxies[i], D.proxies[j]] = [D.proxies[j], D.proxies[i]];
    openSet.clear(); openSet.add(j); save(); renderDev();
  },
  addLink(el) { D.proxies[+el.dataset.i].links.push({ url: "https://", status: "unknown" }); save(); renderDev(); },
  delLink(el) { D.proxies[+el.dataset.i].links.splice(+el.dataset.j, 1); save(); renderDev(); },
  addStr(el) { getPath(D, el.dataset.list).push(""); save(); renderDev(); },
  delStr(el) { getPath(D, el.dataset.list).splice(+el.dataset.j, 1); save(); renderDev(); },
  addImage() {
    const name = (prompt("Name for this picture (letters/numbers, no spaces):") || "").trim().replace(/[^\w-]/g, "");
    if (!name) return;
    if (name in D.images) return toast("That name already exists");
    D.images[name] = ""; save(); renderDev();
  },
  linkImage(el) {
    const url = prompt("Paste the picture's web link:", D.images[el.dataset.key].startsWith("data:") ? "" : D.images[el.dataset.key]);
    if (url === null) return;
    D.images[el.dataset.key] = url.trim(); save(); renderDev();
  },
  delImage(el) {
    const k = el.dataset.key;
    if (!confirm(`Delete picture "${k}"?`)) return;
    delete D.images[k];
    D.proxies.forEach(p => { if (p.image === k) p.image = ""; });
    (D.textPanels || []).forEach(p => { if (p.image === k) p.image = "" });
    save(); renderDev();
  },
  download() {
    const blob = new Blob([buildHTML()], { type: "text/html" });
    const a = document.createElement("a");
    a.href = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    a.download = "ProxyPathz.html";
    document.body.appendChild(a); a.click(); a.remove();
    setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(a.href), 5000);
    toast("Downloading…");
  },
  copyHTML() {
    navigator.clipboard.writeText(buildHTML())
      .then(() => toast("Copied! Paste it into your file."))
      .catch(() => toast("Copy failed, use Download instead"));
  },
  async setPass() {
    const u = $("newUser").value.trim(), p = $("newPass").value;
    if (!u || !p) return toast("Enter a username and a password");
    D.devHash = await hashStr(u + "\n" + p);
    save(); renderDev();
    toast("Login saved. Download the file to keep it.");
  },
  lock() { localStorage.removeItem(UNLOCK_KEY); closeDev(); toast("Locked"); },
  reset() {
    if (!confirm("Throw away all changes saved on this device?")) return;
    localStorage.removeItem(DRAFT_KEY); location.reload();
  }
};

/* ---------- Events ---------- */
document.addEventListener("click", e => {
  // Developer section navigation. Keep this scoped so other widgets can use data-tab too.
  const tabButton = e.target.closest("#devTabs [data-tab]");
  if (tabButton) {
    e.preventDefault();
    tab = tabButton.dataset.tab;
    renderDev();
    $("devBody").scrollTop = 0;
    return;
  }
  const a = e.target.closest("[data-act]");
  if (a) { e.preventDefault(); ACT[a.dataset.act]?.(a); return; }
  const t = e.target.closest(".tile");
  if (t) return openProxy(+t.dataset.i);
  if (e.target.id === "modal" || e.target.classList.contains("close")) closeModal();
});

function onEdit(e) {
  const el = e.target;
  if (!el.dataset.path || !el.closest("#dev")) return;
  let v = el.type === "checkbox" ? el.checked : el.value;
  if (el.dataset.type === "csv") v = v.split(",").map(s => s.trim()).filter(Boolean);
  setPath(D, el.dataset.path, v);
  save();
  if (el.dataset.path.startsWith("textPanels.")) {
    const bits = el.dataset.path.split("."), i = +bits[1];
    const editor = el.closest(".tp-edit-card");
    const preview = editor && editor.querySelector(".tp-live-preview");
    if (preview && D.textPanels[i]) preview.innerHTML = textPanelCard(D.textPanels[i], i, true);
    if (bits[2] === "title" && editor) {
      const summary = editor.querySelector("summary");
      if (summary) summary.textContent = D.textPanels[i]?.title || "(untitled panel)";
    }
  }
}
document.addEventListener("input", onEdit);
document.addEventListener("change", e => {
  onEdit(e);
  const el = e.target;
  if (el.type === "file" && el.dataset.key && el.files[0]) {
    const f = el.files[0];
    if (f.size > 400 * 1024 && !confirm(`This picture is ${Math.round(f.size / 1024)} KB, which will make your file bigger. Use it anyway?`)) return;
    const r = new FileReader();
    r.onload = () => {
      D.images[el.dataset.key] = r.result;
      if (el.dataset.panelIndex !== undefined) {
        const panel = D.textPanels[+el.dataset.panelIndex];
        if (panel) panel.image = el.dataset.key;
      }
      save(); renderDev(); toast("Picture added");
    };
    r.readAsDataURL(f);
  }
});
document.addEventListener("toggle", e => {
  if (!e.target.matches?.("details.card")) return;
  if (e.target.dataset.tpI !== undefined) {
    const i = +e.target.dataset.tpI;
    e.target.open ? textPanelOpenSet.add(i) : textPanelOpenSet.delete(i);
    return;
  }
  const i = +e.target.dataset.i;
  e.target.open ? openSet.add(i) : openSet.delete(i);
}, true);

let keyBuf = "";
document.addEventListener("keydown", e => {
  if (e.key === "Escape") { closeModal(); if ($("dev").classList.contains("open")) closeDev(); }
  if (e.key === "Enter" && e.target.id === "passIn") ACT.checkPass();
  if (e.key === "Enter" && e.target.classList.contains("tile")) openProxy(+e.target.dataset.i);
  if (e.key.length === 1 && !e.target.matches("input,textarea")) {
    keyBuf = (keyBuf + e.key.toLowerCase()).slice(-7);
    if (keyBuf === "devmode") tryOpenDev();
  }
});

// Secret: tap the title 5 times fast
let taps = 0, tapTimer;
$("siteTitle").addEventListener("click", () => {
  taps++; clearTimeout(tapTimer);
  tapTimer = setTimeout(() => taps = 0, 1500);
  if (taps >= 5) { taps = 0; tryOpenDev(); }
});
// Secret: add #dev to the URL
const checkHash = () => { if (location.hash === "#dev") tryOpenDev(); };
window.addEventListener("hashchange", checkHash);

$("search").addEventListener("input", renderAll);

/* ---------- Canvas ---------- */
let selId = null, snap = true, newColor = "#a66bff";
const FONTS = [["Default","inherit"],["Poppins","'Poppins',sans-serif"],["Orbitron","'Orbitron',sans-serif"],
  ["Russo One","'Russo One',sans-serif"],["Space Grotesk","'Space Grotesk',sans-serif"],["JetBrains Mono","'JetBrains Mono',monospace"]];
const hex = (h, a) => { h = /^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(h) ? h : "#a66bff";
  return `rgba(${parseInt(h.slice(1,3),16)},${parseInt(h.slice(3,5),16)},${parseInt(h.slice(5,7),16)},${a})`; };
const editing = () => document.body.classList.contains("editing");
const safeUrl = u => { try { const x = new URL(u); return ["http:","https:"].includes(x.protocol) ? x.href : ""; } catch { return ""; } };
const sel = () => D.canvas.items.find(i => i.id === selId);

function renderCanvas() {
  const C = D.canvas, cv = $("cv");
  $("canvasBox").hidden = !(editing() || C.items.length);
  cv.style.height = (parseInt(C.height) || 520) + "px";
  cv.replaceChildren();
  C.items.forEach(it => {
    const el = document.createElement("div"), s = el.style;
    el.className = "ci" + (it.id === selId && editing() ? " sel" : "");
    el.dataset.id = it.id;
    s.left = it.x + "%"; s.top = it.y + "%"; s.width = it.w + "%"; s.height = it.h + "%";
    s.borderRadius = it.type === "circle" ? "50%" : (+it.radius || 0) + "px";
    s.opacity = (100 - (+it.op || 0)) / 100;
    s.background = hex(it.fill, (it.fillA ?? 100) / 100);
    s.color = /^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(it.color) ? it.color : "#ffffff";
    s.fontSize = (+it.size || 18) + "px";
    s.fontFamily = (FONTS.find(f => f[0] === it.font) || FONTS[0])[1];
    const src = img(it.image); if (src) s.backgroundImage = `url("${src.replace(/"/g, "%22")}")`;
    if (it.link) s.cursor = "pointer";
    el.textContent = it.text || "";
    const rh = document.createElement("i"); rh.className = "rh"; el.appendChild(rh);
    cv.appendChild(el);
  });
}

function renderEd() {
  const p = $("edit"); p.hidden = !editing(); if (p.hidden) return;
  const it = sel();
  const rng = (k, label) => `<label class="field"><span>${label}</span><input type="range" min="0" max="100" data-k="${k}" value="${it[k] ?? 0}"></label>`;
  const num = (k, label) => `<label class="field"><span>${label}</span><input type="number" data-k="${k}" value="${it[k] ?? 0}"></label>`;
  const col = (k, label) => `<label class="field"><span>${label}</span><input type="color" data-k="${k}" value="${esc(it[k])}"></label>`;
  p.innerHTML = `
    <div class="row">
      <button class="btn sm" data-act="edAdd" data-t="rect">+ Rectangle</button>
      <button class="btn sm" data-act="edAdd" data-t="circle">+ Circle</button>
      <button class="btn sm" data-act="edAdd" data-t="text">+ Text</button>
      <button class="btn sm" data-act="edAdd" data-t="button">+ Button</button>
    </div>
    <div class="row">
      <input type="color" id="newColor" value="${newColor}" style="width:56px;flex:0 0 56px;height:44px;padding:2px">
      <label class="check" style="margin:0;flex:1"><input type="checkbox" id="snapT" ${snap ? "checked" : ""}> Grid snap</label>
      <button class="btn sm" data-act="undo">↶ Undo</button>
      <button class="btn sm pri" data-act="edDone">Done</button>
    </div>` + (!it ? `<p class="sub">Tap an item to edit it. Drag to move it, drag the corner square to resize.</p>` : `
    <label class="field"><span>Text</span><textarea data-k="text">${esc(it.text)}</textarea></label>
    ${num("size", "Font size")}${col("color", "Text color")}
    <label class="field"><span>Font</span><select data-k="font">${FONTS.map(f => `<option ${f[0] === it.font ? "selected" : ""}>${f[0]}</option>`).join("")}</select></label>
    ${col("fill", "Fill color")}${rng("fillA", "Background opacity")}${rng("op", "Transparency")}${num("radius", "Corner radius (px)")}
    <label class="field"><span>Picture</span><select data-k="image"><option value="">(none)</option>${Object.keys(D.images).map(k => `<option value="${esc(k)}" ${k === it.image ? "selected" : ""}>${esc(k)}</option>`).join("")}</select></label>

    <div class="row"><button class="btn sm" data-act="edFwd">↑ Forward</button><button class="btn sm" data-act="edBack">↓ Backward</button>
      <button class="btn sm danger" data-act="edDel" style="margin-left:auto">Delete</button></div>`);
}
function edMove(d) {
  const a = D.canvas.items, i = a.findIndex(x => x.id === selId), j = i + d;
  if (i < 0 || j < 0 || j >= a.length) return;
  [a[i], a[j]] = [a[j], a[i]]; save(); renderCanvas();
}
$("edit").addEventListener("input", e => {
  const el = e.target;
  if (el.id === "newColor") { newColor = el.value; return; }
  if (el.id === "snapT") { snap = el.checked; return; }
  const it = sel(); if (!it || !el.dataset.k) return;
  it[el.dataset.k] = (el.type === "range" || el.type === "number") ? +el.value : el.value;
  save();
});

let drag = null;
$("cv").addEventListener("pointerdown", e => { if (drag) return;
  if (!editing()) return;
  const el = e.target.closest(".ci"), it = el && D.canvas.items.find(i => i.id === el.dataset.id);
  if (!it) return;
  e.preventDefault();
  const changed = selId !== it.id; selId = it.id;
  $("cv").querySelectorAll(".ci.sel").forEach(x => x.classList.remove("sel")); el.classList.add("sel");
  drag = { id: e.pointerId, moved: false, it, el, rs: e.target.classList.contains("rh"), x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY,
    ox: it.x, oy: it.y, ow: it.w, oh: it.h, r: $("cv").getBoundingClientRect() };
  el.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);
  if (changed) renderEd();
});
document.addEventListener("pointermove", e => {
  if (!drag || e.pointerId !== drag.id) return; drag.moved = true;
  const dx = (e.clientX - drag.x) / drag.r.width * 100, dy = (e.clientY - drag.y) / drag.r.height * 100;
  const q = v => snap ? Math.round(v / 5) * 5 : Math.round(v * 10) / 10, { it, el } = drag;
  if (drag.rs) { it.w = Math.max(5, Math.min(100 - it.x, q(drag.ow + dx))); it.h = Math.max(5, Math.min(100 - it.y, q(drag.oh + dy))); }
  else { it.x = Math.max(0, Math.min(100 - it.w, q(drag.ox + dx))); it.y = Math.max(0, Math.min(100 - it.h, q(drag.oy + dy))); }
  el.style.left = it.x + "%"; el.style.top = it.y + "%"; el.style.width = it.w + "%"; el.style.height = it.h + "%";
});
const endDrag = e => { if (!drag || e.pointerId !== drag.id) return; const m = drag.moved; drag = null; if (m) save(); };
document.addEventListener("pointerup", endDrag);
document.addEventListener("pointercancel", endDrag);

/* ---------- Guest canvas: shapes + text only, locked inside a 540px box, saved per device ---------- */
const GKEY = "ppz_guest", GMAX = 30;
const clamp = (v, a, b) => Math.max(a, Math.min(b, +v || 0));
const hexOk = (h, d) => /^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(h) ? h : d;
function cleanItem(it) {   // used for guest drawings and for codes the owner imports
  if (!it || !["rect", "circle", "text"].includes(it.type)) return null;
  const w = clamp(it.w, 5, 100), h = clamp(it.h, 5, 100);
  return { id: "g" + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 9), type: it.type,
    x: clamp(it.x, 0, 100 - w), y: clamp(it.y, 0, 100 - h), w, h,
    text: String(it.text || "").slice(0, 60), size: clamp(it.size || 18, 10, 48),
    color: hexOk(it.color, "#ffffff"), fill: hexOk(it.fill, "#a66bff"),
    fillA: it.type === "text" ? 0 : 100, op: 0, radius: 0, image: "", font: "Default", link: "" };
}
let G = [], gSel = null, gNew = "#a66bff", gDrag = null;
try { G = (JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(GKEY)) || []).slice(0, GMAX).map(cleanItem).filter(Boolean); } catch (e) {}
const gSave = () => { try { localStorage.setItem(GKEY, JSON.stringify(G)); } catch (e) {} };

function paintGuest() {
  const gv = $("gv"); gv.replaceChildren();
  G.forEach(it => {
    const el = document.createElement("div"), st2 = el.style;
    el.className = "ci" + (it.id === gSel ? " sel" : ""); el.dataset.id = it.id;
    st2.left = it.x + "%"; st2.top = it.y + "%"; st2.width = it.w + "%"; st2.height = it.h + "%";
    st2.borderRadius = it.type === "circle" ? "50%" : "0";
    st2.background = hex(it.fill, it.fillA / 100); st2.color = it.color; st2.fontSize = it.size + "px";
    el.textContent = it.text;
    const rh = document.createElement("i"); rh.className = "rh"; el.appendChild(rh); gv.appendChild(el);
  });
}
function toolsGuest() {
  const it = G.find(i => i.id === gSel);
  $("gtools").innerHTML = `<div class="row" style="margin-top:12px;flex-wrap:wrap">
      <button class="btn sm" data-act="gAdd" data-t="rect">+ Rectangle</button>
      <button class="btn sm" data-act="gAdd" data-t="circle">+ Circle</button>
      <button class="btn sm" data-act="gAdd" data-t="text">+ Text</button>
      <input type="color" id="gNew" value="${gNew}" style="width:56px;flex:0 0 56px;height:44px;padding:2px"></div>` +
    (it ? `<label class="field"><span>Text (max 60 letters)</span><input data-g="text" maxlength="60" value="${esc(it.text)}"></label>
      <label class="field"><span>Font size</span><input type="range" min="10" max="48" data-g="size" value="${it.size}"></label>
      <div class="row"><label class="field" style="flex:1"><span>Fill</span><input type="color" data-g="fill" value="${esc(it.fill)}"></label>
        <label class="field" style="flex:1"><span>Text color</span><input type="color" data-g="color" value="${esc(it.color)}"></label></div>
      <button class="btn sm danger" data-act="gDel">Delete item</button>`
    : `<p class="sub">Tap an item to edit it. Drag to move it, drag the corner square to resize.</p>`) +
    `<div class="row" style="margin-top:8px"><button class="btn sm" data-act="gCopy">📋 Copy my canvas</button>
      <button class="btn sm danger" data-act="gClear" style="margin-left:auto">Clear all</button></div>`;
}
function renderGuest() { paintGuest(); toolsGuest(); }
$("gtools").addEventListener("input", e => {
  const el = e.target;
  if (el.id === "gNew") { gNew = hexOk(el.value, gNew); return; }
  const it = G.find(i => i.id === gSel), k = el.dataset.g; if (!it || !k) return;
  it[k] = k === "text" ? el.value.slice(0, 60) : k === "size" ? clamp(el.value, 10, 48) : hexOk(el.value, it[k]);
  gSave(); paintGuest();
});
$("gv").addEventListener("pointerdown", e => { if (gDrag) return;
  const el = e.target.closest(".ci"), it = el && G.find(i => i.id === el.dataset.id); if (!it) return;
  e.preventDefault();
  const changed = gSel !== it.id; gSel = it.id;
  $("gv").querySelectorAll(".ci.sel").forEach(x => x.classList.remove("sel")); el.classList.add("sel");
  gDrag = { id: e.pointerId, it, el, rs: e.target.classList.contains("rh"), x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY,
    ox: it.x, oy: it.y, ow: it.w, oh: it.h, r: $("gv").getBoundingClientRect() };
  el.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);
  if (changed) toolsGuest();
});
document.addEventListener("pointermove", e => {
  if (!gDrag || e.pointerId !== gDrag.id) return;
  const d = gDrag, it = d.it, dx = (e.clientX - d.x) / d.r.width * 100, dy = (e.clientY - d.y) / d.r.height * 100;

  const q = d.el.style; q.left = it.x + "%"; q.top = it.y + "%"; q.width = it.w + "%"; q.height = it.h + "%";
});
const gEnd = e => { if (gDrag && e.pointerId === gDrag.id) { gDrag = null; gSave(); } };
document.addEventListener("pointerup", gEnd);
document.addEventListener("pointercancel", gEnd);
renderGuest();

        const f = inp.files[0]; if (!f) return;
        const r = new FileReader();
        r.onload = () => {
          let d;
          try { const j = JSON.parse(r.result); d = j && j.data ? j.data : j; } catch (e) { d = null; }
          if (!d || !Array.isArray(d.proxies) || !d.site) return toast("That file isn't a ProxyPathz backup");
          if (!confirm(`Replace everything in the editor with this backup (${d.proxies.length} proxies)?`)) return;

          if (typeof renderEd === "function") renderEd();
          renderFavs();

        };
        r.readAsText(f);
      };
      inp.click();
    };
