      const rs = document.documentElement.style, fs = rs.fontSize, spd = rs.getPropertyValue("--spd");
      rs.removeProperty("font-size"); rs.removeProperty("--spd");
      try { return baseBuild.apply(this, arguments); }
      finally {
        moved.reverse().forEach(([e, p, n]) => p.insertBefore(e, n && n.parentNode === p ? n : null));
        had.forEach(c => b.add(c)); if (big) h.add("vbig");
        if (fs) rs.fontSize = fs; if (spd) rs.setProperty("--spd", spd);
      }
    };
  }
})();
</script>
<script id="ppz-addon-background">
/* ===== ProxyPathz add-on: animated backgrounds + background pictures ===== */
(() => {
  if (window.__ppzBg) return; window.__ppzBg = true;
  const BKEY = "proxypathz_v15_bg";
  const SP = () => window.__ppzSpeed || 1;
  const STYLES = [["grid", "Classic grid"], ["aurora", "Aurora glow"], ["stars", "Starfield"], ["neon", "Neon horizon"], ["plain", "Plain color"]];
  const OK = STYLES.map(s => s[0]);
  let VB = { style: "default", img: "", dim: 45 };
  try { VB = Object.assign(VB, JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(BKEY)) || {}); } catch (e) {}
  const saveVB = () => {
    try { localStorage.setItem(BKEY, JSON.stringify(VB)); return true; }
    catch (e) { toast("That picture is too big for this device. Try a smaller one."); return false; }
  };
  const siteBg = () => (D.site.bg && typeof D.site.bg === "object") ? D.site.bg : {};
  const motionOff = () => document.body.classList.contains("noanim") || document.body.classList.contains("vnoanim") ||
    matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)").matches;

  /* ---------- Styles ---------- */
  document.getElementById("ppzBgCss")?.remove();
  const css = document.createElement("style");
  css.id = "ppzBgCss"; css.setAttribute("data-ppzv", "");
  css.textContent = `
html.ppz-bgon body { animation:none !important; background-image:none !important; }
#ppzBg { position:fixed; inset:0; z-index:-1; pointer-events:none; overflow:hidden; display:none; background:var(--bg); }
#ppzBg.on { display:block; }
#ppzBg > * { position:absolute; inset:0; }
#ppzBg .pic { display:none; background-size:cover; background-position:center; animation:ppzZoom 40s ease-in-out infinite alternate; }
#ppzBg.has-pic .pic { display:block; }
#ppzBg .dim { display:none; background:var(--bg); }
#ppzBg.has-pic .dim { display:block; }
#ppzBg .fx { display:none; }
#ppzBg canvas { display:none; width:100%; height:100%; }
#ppzBg.s-stars canvas { display:block; }
@keyframes ppzZoom { to { transform:scale(1.08); } }

#ppzBg.s-grid .fx { display:block; background-image:linear-gradient(var(--line) 1px,transparent 1px),
  linear-gradient(90deg,var(--line) 1px,transparent 1px); background-size:40px 40px; opacity:.6; animation:drift 20s linear infinite; }

#ppzBg.s-aurora .fx { display:block; background-image:linear-gradient(rgba(58,34,102,.35) 1px,transparent 1px),
  linear-gradient(90deg,rgba(58,34,102,.35) 1px,transparent 1px); background-size:40px 40px; }
#ppzBg.s-aurora .fx::before, #ppzBg.s-aurora .fx::after { content:""; position:absolute; width:70vmax; height:70vmax;
  border-radius:50%; will-change:transform; }
#ppzBg.s-aurora .fx::before { left:-20vmax; top:-25vmax; opacity:.55;
  background:radial-gradient(circle,var(--accent) 0%,transparent 62%); animation:ppzFloatA 22s ease-in-out infinite alternate; }
#ppzBg.s-aurora .fx::after { right:-25vmax; bottom:-30vmax; opacity:.35;
  background:radial-gradient(circle,#ff5cc8 0%,transparent 60%); animation:ppzFloatB 28s ease-in-out infinite alternate; }
@keyframes ppzFloatA { to { transform:translate(30vw,25vh) scale(1.2); } }
@keyframes ppzFloatB { to { transform:translate(-35vw,-20vh) scale(.85); } }

#ppzBg.s-neon .fx { display:block; background:linear-gradient(to bottom,var(--bg) 0%,#2b0f52 49.5%,var(--accent) 50%,var(--bg) 50.6%); }
#ppzBg.s-neon .fx::before { content:""; position:absolute; left:50%; top:50%; width:44vmin; height:44vmin; border-radius:50%;
  transform:translate(-50%,-92%); opacity:.85; background:linear-gradient(#ffd36b,#ff5cc8 55%,var(--accent));
  -webkit-mask:repeating-linear-gradient(to bottom,#000 0 16px,transparent 16px 20px);
  mask:repeating-linear-gradient(to bottom,#000 0 16px,transparent 16px 20px); }
#ppzBg.s-neon .fx::after { content:""; position:absolute; left:-50%; width:200%; top:50%; height:100%;
  background-image:linear-gradient(var(--accent) 2px,transparent 2px),linear-gradient(90deg,var(--accent) 2px,transparent 2px);
  background-size:60px 60px; opacity:.55; transform-origin:top; transform:perspective(260px) rotateX(70deg);
  animation:ppzRoad 1.6s linear infinite; }
@keyframes ppzRoad { to { background-position:0 60px; } }

body { animation-duration:calc(20s / var(--spd, 1)); }
#ppzBg .pic { animation-duration:calc(40s / var(--spd, 1)); }
#ppzBg.s-grid .fx { animation-duration:calc(20s / var(--spd, 1)); }
#ppzBg.s-aurora .fx::before { animation-duration:calc(22s / var(--spd, 1)); }
#ppzBg.s-aurora .fx::after { animation-duration:calc(28s / var(--spd, 1)); }
#ppzBg.s-neon .fx::after { animation-duration:calc(1.6s / var(--spd, 1)); }
#ppzSet select { background:var(--panel2); color:var(--text); border:1px solid var(--line); border-radius:8px;
  padding:8px; font:inherit; font-size:16px; max-width:150px; }
#ppzSet input[type=range] { width:130px; accent-color:var(--accent); }
#ppzSet .row button:disabled { opacity:.4; cursor:default; }
#ppzBgOwner select { width:100%; padding:9px; border-radius:8px; border:1px solid var(--line);
  background:var(--panel2); color:var(--text); font:inherit; font-size:16px; }
#ppzBgOwner input[type=range] { width:100%; accent-color:var(--accent); }`;
  document.head.appendChild(css);

  /* ---------- Background layer ---------- */
  document.getElementById("ppzBg")?.remove();
  const layer = document.createElement("div");
  layer.id = "ppzBg"; layer.setAttribute("data-ppzv", ""); layer.setAttribute("aria-hidden", "true");
  layer.innerHTML = '<div class="pic"></div><div class="dim"></div><div class="fx"></div><canvas></canvas>';
  document.body.prepend(layer);
  const pic = layer.querySelector(".pic"), dim = layer.querySelector(".dim");
  const cv = layer.querySelector("canvas"), ctx = cv.getContext("2d");

  /* ---------- Starfield ---------- */
  let stars = [], shoot = null, raf = 0, last = 0, W = 0, H = 0, starsOn = false, wasOff = null, starColor = "#a66bff";
  function sizeStars() {
    const dpr = Math.min(devicePixelRatio || 1, 2);
    W = innerWidth; H = innerHeight;
    cv.width = W * dpr; cv.height = H * dpr; ctx.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, 0, 0);
    const n = Math.min(220, Math.round(W * H / 5000));
    stars = Array.from({ length: n }, () => ({ x: Math.random() * W, y: Math.random() * H, r: Math.random() * 1.4 + .3,
      p: Math.random() * 6.28, s: Math.random() * 1.5 + .5, v: Math.random() * .15 + .03 }));
  }
  function drawStars(t, move) {
    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, W, H);
    for (const s of stars) {
      if (move) { s.y += s.v * SP(); if (s.y > H) { s.y = 0; s.x = Math.random() * W; } }
      ctx.globalAlpha = .35 + .65 * Math.abs(Math.sin(s.p + t / 1000 * s.s * SP()));
      ctx.fillStyle = s.r > 1.35 ? starColor : "#fff";
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(s.x, s.y, s.r, 0, 6.283); ctx.fill();
    }
    if (!move) return;
    if (!shoot && Math.random() < .004 * SP()) shoot = { x: Math.random() * W * .7, y: Math.random() * H * .4, life: 1 };
    if (shoot) {
      const g = ctx.createLinearGradient(shoot.x, shoot.y, shoot.x - 90, shoot.y - 40);
      g.addColorStop(0, "rgba(255,255,255," + shoot.life + ")"); g.addColorStop(1, "rgba(255,255,255,0)");
      ctx.globalAlpha = 1; ctx.strokeStyle = g; ctx.lineWidth = 2;
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(shoot.x, shoot.y); ctx.lineTo(shoot.x - 90, shoot.y - 40); ctx.stroke();
      shoot.x += 10 * SP(); shoot.y += 4.5 * SP(); shoot.life -= .03 * SP();
      if (shoot.life <= 0) shoot = null;
    }
  }
  function loop(t) { raf = requestAnimationFrame(loop); if (t - last < 33) return; last = t; drawStars(t, true); }
  function stopStars() { cancelAnimationFrame(raf); raf = 0; }
  function startStars() {
    stopStars(); sizeStars(); wasOff = motionOff();
    if (wasOff || document.hidden) { drawStars(0, false); return; }
    raf = requestAnimationFrame(loop);
  }
  document.addEventListener("visibilitychange", () => {
    if (!starsOn) return;
    if (document.hidden) stopStars(); else if (!motionOff() && !raf) raf = requestAnimationFrame(loop);
  });
  let rz = 0;
  addEventListener("resize", () => {
    if (!starsOn) return;
    clearTimeout(rz);
    rz = setTimeout(() => { if (Math.abs(innerWidth - W) > 2 || Math.abs(innerHeight - H) > 120) startStars(); }, 250);
  });
  new MutationObserver(() => { if (starsOn && motionOff() !== wasOff) startStars(); })
    .observe(document.body, { attributes: true, attributeFilter: ["class"] });

  /* ---------- Apply ---------- */
  let curImg = null;
  function effective() {
    const sb = siteBg();
    let style = VB.style && VB.style !== "default" ? VB.style : (sb.style || "grid");
    if (!OK.includes(style)) style = "grid";
    const img = VB.img || sb.img || "";
    const d = VB.img ? VB.dim : (sb.dim ?? 45);
    return { style, img, dim: Math.max(0, Math.min(85, +d || 0)) };
  }
  function applyBg() {
    const e = effective(), on = !!e.img || e.style !== "grid";
    layer.className = on ? "on s-" + e.style + (e.img ? " has-pic" : "") : "";
    document.documentElement.classList.toggle("ppz-bgon", on);
    if (e.img !== curImg) { pic.style.backgroundImage = e.img ? 'url("' + e.img.replace(/"/g, "%22") + '")' : ""; curImg = e.img; }
    dim.style.opacity = e.img ? e.dim / 100 : 0;
    starColor = getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).getPropertyValue("--accent").trim() || "#a66bff";
    const want = on && e.style === "stars";
    if (want && !starsOn) { starsOn = true; startStars(); }
    else if (!want && starsOn) { starsOn = false; stopStars(); }
  }
  const basePage = renderPage;
  renderPage = function () { basePage.apply(this, arguments); applyBg(); };
  applyBg();

  /* ---------- Picture helpers ---------- */
  function pickFile(cb) {
    const inp = document.createElement("input");
    inp.type = "file"; inp.accept = "image/*";
    inp.onchange = () => inp.files[0] && cb(inp.files[0]);
    inp.click();
  }
  function readPic(file, max) {
    return new Promise((res, rej) => {
      if (!file.type.startsWith("image/")) return rej("not an image");
      const fr = new FileReader();
      fr.onerror = rej;
      fr.onload = () => {
        if (file.type === "image/gif" && file.size < 1.5e6) return res(fr.result);
        const im = new Image();
        im.onload = () => {
          const k = Math.min(1, max / Math.max(im.width, im.height));
          const c = document.createElement("canvas");
          c.width = Math.round(im.width * k); c.height = Math.round(im.height * k);
          c.getContext("2d").drawImage(im, 0, 0, c.width, c.height);
          res(c.toDataURL("image/jpeg", .8));
        };
        im.onerror = rej;
        im.src = fr.result;
      };
      fr.readAsDataURL(file);
    });
  }
  const opt = (sel) => STYLES.map(([v, l]) => `<option value="${v}" ${sel === v ? "selected" : ""}>${l}</option>`).join("");

  /* ---------- Visitor: ⚙ Settings section ---------- */
  const panel = $("ppzSet");
  function addVisitor() {
    if (!panel || !panel.lastElementChild || panel.querySelector("#ppzVBgSec")) return;
    const sec = document.createElement("div");
    sec.id = "ppzVBgSec";
    sec.innerHTML = `<h3 style="margin-top:12px">🌌 Background</h3>
      <label><span>Style<small>Just for you</small></span>
        <select id="ppzVStyle"><option value="default" ${VB.style === "default" ? "selected" : ""}>Site default</option>${opt(VB.style)}</select></label>
      <div class="row" style="margin-top:8px">
        <button type="button" id="ppzVUp">⬆ My picture</button>
        <button type="button" id="ppzVDel" ${VB.img ? "" : "disabled"}>Remove picture</button></div>
      ${VB.img ? `<label><span>Darken picture<small id="ppzVDimV">${VB.dim}%</small></span>
        <input type="range" id="ppzVDim" min="0" max="85" value="${VB.dim}"></label>` : ""}`;
    panel.insertBefore(sec, panel.lastElementChild);
    const redo = () => { sec.remove(); addVisitor(); };
    $("ppzVStyle").onchange = e => { VB.style = e.target.value; saveVB(); applyBg(); };
    $("ppzVUp").onclick = () => pickFile(async f => {
      try {
        const old = VB.img; VB.img = await readPic(f, 1400);
        if (!saveVB()) VB.img = old;
        applyBg(); redo();
      } catch (e) { toast("Couldn't read that picture"); }
    });
    $("ppzVDel").onclick = () => { VB.img = ""; saveVB(); applyBg(); redo(); };
    const dr = $("ppzVDim");
    if (dr) {
      dr.oninput = () => { VB.dim = +dr.value; $("ppzVDimV").textContent = dr.value + "%"; applyBg(); };
      dr.onchange = saveVB;
    }
  }
  if (panel) { new MutationObserver(addVisitor).observe(panel, { childList: true }); addVisitor(); }

  /* ---------- Owner: Look tab section ---------- */
  if (typeof PUBLIC_BUILD !== "undefined" && !PUBLIC_BUILD && typeof renderDev === "function") {
    const escA = s => String(s).replace(/&/g, "&amp;").replace(/"/g, "&quot;").replace(/</g, "&lt;");
    const baseDev = renderDev;
    renderDev = function () { baseDev.apply(this, arguments); addOwner(); };

    function addOwner() {
      const body = $("devBody");
      if (!body || !body.querySelector('[data-path="site.accent"]') || body.querySelector("#ppzBgOwner")) return;
      const sb = siteBg(), d = sb.dim ?? 45, img = sb.img || "";
      const isData = img.startsWith("data:");
      const osp = +D.site.bgSpeed > 0 ? +D.site.bgSpeed : 1;
      const info = !img ? "No picture yet."
        : isData ? `Uploaded picture is stored inside your file (${Math.round(img.length / 1024)} KB).`
        : "Using a picture link.";
      const sec = document.createElement("div");
      sec.id = "ppzBgOwner";
      sec.innerHTML = `
        <h3 style="margin-top:18px">🌌 Background</h3>
        <p class="sub">This is what everyone sees. Visitors can still pick their own in ⚙ Settings.</p>
        <label class="field"><span>Animated style</span>
          <select id="ppzOStyle">${opt(sb.style || "grid")}</select></label>
        <label class="field"><span>Animation speed: <b id="ppzOSpdV">${+osp.toFixed(2)}×</b></span>
          <input type="range" id="ppzOSpd" min="25" max="300" step="25" value="${Math.round(osp * 100)}"></label>
        <label class="field"><span>Picture link (best option, keeps your file small)</span>
          <input id="ppzOLink" placeholder="images/background.jpg" value="${isData ? "" : escA(img)}"></label>
        <div class="row" style="margin:8px 0">
          <button type="button" class="btn sm" id="ppzOUp">⬆ Upload picture</button>
          <button type="button" class="btn sm danger" id="ppzODel" ${img ? "" : "disabled"}>Remove picture</button></div>
        <p class="sub">${info}</p>
        ${img ? `<label class="field"><span>Darken picture: <b id="ppzODimV">${d}%</b></span>
          <input type="range" id="ppzODim" min="0" max="85" value="${d}"></label>` : ""}`;
      body.appendChild(sec);

      const redraw = () => { sec.remove(); addOwner(); };
      const setSite = patch => { D.site.bg = Object.assign({}, siteBg(), patch); save(); applyBg(); };

      $("ppzOStyle").onchange = e => setSite({ style: e.target.value });
      const sd = $("ppzOSpd");
      sd.oninput = () => { D.site.bgSpeed = +sd.value / 100; $("ppzOSpdV").textContent = D.site.bgSpeed + "×"; window.__ppzApplyV && window.__ppzApplyV(); };
      sd.onchange = () => save();
      $("ppzOLink").onchange = e => { setSite({ img: e.target.value.trim() }); redraw(); };
      $("ppzOUp").onclick = () => pickFile(async f => {
        try {
          const data = await readPic(f, 1600);
          setSite({ img: data });
          toast("Background set (" + Math.round(data.length / 1024) + " KB added to your file)");
          redraw();
        } catch (e) { toast("Couldn't read that picture"); }
      });
      $("ppzODel").onclick = () => { setSite({ img: "" }); redraw(); };
      const dr = $("ppzODim");
      if (dr) {
        dr.oninput = () => {
          $("ppzODimV").textContent = dr.value + "%";
          D.site.bg = Object.assign({}, siteBg(), { dim: +dr.value });
          applyBg();
        };
        dr.onchange = () => save();
      }
    }
    addOwner();
  }
})();
</script>
<script id="ppz-addon-cards">
/* ===== ProxyPathz add-on: custom card title bars ===== */
(() => {
  if (window.__ppzCards) return; window.__ppzCards = true;
  const CAPS = [["default", "Default"], ["glass", "Glass"], ["solid", "Solid color"], ["gradient", "Gradient"],
    ["animated", "Animated gradient"], ["neon", "Neon glow"], ["shine", "Shine"], ["picture", "Picture"], ["none", "None (text only)"]];
  const CAP_OK = CAPS.map(c => c[0]);
  const TXT = [["auto", "Normal"], ["light", "White"], ["dark", "Dark"]];
  const KEYS = ["capStyle", "capC1", "capC2", "capText", "capImage"];

  /* ---------- Styles ---------- */
  document.getElementById("ppzCardsCss")?.remove();
  const css = document.createElement("style");
  css.id = "ppzCardsCss"; css.setAttribute("data-ppzv", "");
  css.textContent = `
.tile .info.cs-glass { background:rgba(29,15,51,.42) !important; backdrop-filter:blur(10px); -webkit-backdrop-filter:blur(10px);
  border-top:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.14); }
.tile .info.cs-solid { background:var(--c1) !important; }
.tile .info.cs-gradient { background:linear-gradient(135deg,var(--c1),var(--c2)) !important; }
.tile .info.cs-animated { background:linear-gradient(120deg,var(--c1),var(--c2),var(--c1)) !important;
  background-size:300% 100% !important; animation:ppzCapFlow 6s ease-in-out infinite; }
@keyframes ppzCapFlow { 0%,100% { background-position:0% 50%; } 50% { background-position:100% 50%; } }
.tile .info.cs-neon { background:rgba(10,4,20,.85) !important; border-top:2px solid var(--c1);
  box-shadow:0 -6px 18px -6px var(--c1); }
.tile .info.cs-neon h3 { color:#fff; text-shadow:0 0 6px var(--c1),0 0 14px var(--c1); }
.tile .info.cs-shine { background:linear-gradient(110deg,transparent 35%,rgba(255,255,255,.28) 50%,transparent 65%),
  rgba(29,15,51,.5) !important; background-size:250% 100%,auto !important; backdrop-filter:blur(8px);
  -webkit-backdrop-filter:blur(8px); animation:ppzCapShine 3.5s linear infinite; }
@keyframes ppzCapShine { from { background-position:150% 0,0 0; } to { background-position:-50% 0,0 0; } }
.tile .info.cs-picture { background:linear-gradient(rgba(18,8,31,.55),rgba(18,8,31,.55)),var(--ci) center/cover !important; }
.tile .info.cs-none { background:transparent !important; box-shadow:none; border:none; }
.tile .info.cs-none h3, .tile .info.cs-none small { text-shadow:0 1px 4px rgba(0,0,0,.9),0 0 2px #000; }
.tile .info.ct-light h3, .tile .info.ct-light small { color:#fff !important; }
.tile .info.ct-dark h3, .tile .info.ct-dark small { color:#12081f !important; text-shadow:none !important; }
.ppzCapPrev { pointer-events:none; max-width:230px; margin:8px 0 14px; }
.ppzCapPrev .tile { animation:none !important; }`;
  document.head.appendChild(css);

  /* ---------- Add the style to every card ---------- */
  const baseTile = tile;
  tile = function (p, i) {
    const html = baseTile.apply(this, arguments);
    if (!p) return html;
    const S = D.site || {};
    let s = p.capStyle || S.capStyle || "default";
    if (!CAP_OK.includes(s)) s = "default";
    const c1 = p.capC1 || S.capC1 || "#a66bff";
    const c2 = p.capC2 || S.capC2 || "#2b6bff";
    const txt = p.capText || S.capText || "auto";
    let vars = `--c1:${c1};--c2:${c2};`;
    if (s === "picture") {
      const src = img(p.capImage || S.capImage || "");
      if (src) vars += `--ci:url("${src}");`; else s = "glass";
    }
    return html.replace('<div class="info">', `<div class="info cs-${s} ct-${txt}" style="${esc(vars)}">`);
  };

  /* ---------- Developer panel controls ---------- */
  if (typeof PUBLIC_BUILD !== "undefined" && PUBLIC_BUILD) { renderPage(); return; }
  const picOpts = () => [["", "(none)"], ...Object.keys(D.images || {}).map(k => [k, k])];

  function fields(base, o, isProxy) {
    const S = D.site || {};
    const styleList = isProxy ? [["", "Use site style"], ...CAPS] : CAPS;
    const txtList = isProxy ? [["", "Use site text color"], ...TXT] : TXT;
    const c1 = o.capC1 || S.capC1 || "#a66bff", c2 = o.capC2 || S.capC2 || "#2b6bff";
    return `
      <label class="field"><span>Style</span>
        <select data-path="${base}.capStyle">${opts(styleList, o.capStyle || (isProxy ? "" : "default"))}</select></label>
      <div class="row">
        <label class="field" style="flex:1"><span>Color 1</span>
          <input type="color" data-path="${base}.capC1" value="${esc(c1)}" style="height:44px;padding:4px"></label>
        <label class="field" style="flex:1"><span>Color 2</span>
          <input type="color" data-path="${base}.capC2" value="${esc(c2)}" style="height:44px;padding:4px"></label>
      </div>
      <label class="field"><span>Text color</span>
        <select data-path="${base}.capText">${opts(txtList, o.capText || (isProxy ? "" : "auto"))}</select></label>
      <label class="field"><span>Picture (for the "Picture" style)</span>
        <select data-path="${base}.capImage">${opts(picOpts(), o.capImage || "")}</select></label>
      <p class="sub">Colors are used by Solid, Gradient, Animated and Neon.</p>`;
  }

  function drawPreviews() {
    document.querySelectorAll("#devBody .ppzCapPrev").forEach(box => {
      const i = +box.dataset.i, p = D.proxies[i];
      if (!p) { box.innerHTML = `<p class="sub">Add a proxy to see a preview.</p>`; return; }
      const keep = tileAnim; tileAnim = false;
      box.innerHTML = `<div class="grid">${tile(p, i)}</div>`;
      tileAnim = keep;
    });
  }

  function addControls() {
    const body = $("devBody"); if (!body) return;
    if (tab === "look" && !body.querySelector("#ppzCapSite")) {
      const sec = document.createElement("div");
      sec.id = "ppzCapSite";
      sec.innerHTML = `<h3 style="margin-top:18px">🏷 Card title bars</h3>
        <p class="sub">The bar with the name and "click to see more" on every card. Each proxy can override this in the Proxies tab.</p>
        ${fields("site", D.site, false)}
        <div class="ppzCapPrev" data-i="0"></div>`;
      body.appendChild(sec);
    }
    if (tab === "proxies") {
      body.querySelectorAll("details.card[data-i]").forEach(card => {
        if (card.querySelector(".ppzCap")) return;
        const i = +card.dataset.i, p = D.proxies[i], inner = card.querySelector(".in");
        if (!p || !inner) return;
        const box = document.createElement("div");
        box.className = "ppzCap";
        box.innerHTML = `<h3>🏷 Title bar</h3>${fields("proxies." + i, p, true)}
          <div class="ppzCapPrev" data-i="${i}"></div>
          <button class="btn sm" data-act="ppzCapReset" data-i="${i}" style="margin-bottom:12px">Reset to site style</button>`;
        const links = [...inner.querySelectorAll("h3")].find(h => h.textContent.trim() === "Links");
        links ? inner.insertBefore(box, links) : inner.appendChild(box);
      });
    }
    drawPreviews();
  }

  ACT.ppzCapReset = el => {
    const p = D.proxies[+el.dataset.i]; if (!p) return;
    KEYS.forEach(k => delete p[k]);
    save(); renderDev(); renderPage();
    toast("Title bar reset");
  };

  const baseDev = renderDev;
  renderDev = function () { baseDev.apply(this, arguments); addControls(); };

  const onCapEdit = e => {
    const path = e.target.dataset && e.target.dataset.path;
    if (path && /\.cap(Style|C1|C2|Text|Image)$/.test(path)) setTimeout(drawPreviews, 0);
  };
  document.addEventListener("input", onCapEdit);
  document.addEventListener("change", onCapEdit);

  renderPage();
  if ($("dev").classList.contains("open")) renderDev();
})();
</script>
<script id="ppz-addon-publish">
/* ===== ProxyPathz add-on: Publish to GitHub ===== */
(() => {
  if (window.__ppzPub) return; window.__ppzPub = true;
  if (typeof PUBLIC_BUILD !== "undefined" && PUBLIC_BUILD) return;

  const CKEY = "proxypathz_v15_pub", TKEY = "proxypathz_v15_pubtoken";
  let C = { owner: "christopher806", repo: "Proxy-Path", branch: "main", path: "index.html", sv: true, remember: false, last: 0 };
  try { C = Object.assign(C, JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(CKEY)) || {}); } catch (e) {}
  const saveC = () => { try { localStorage.setItem(CKEY, JSON.stringify(C)); } catch (e) {} };
  const getToken = () => sessionStorage.getItem(TKEY) || localStorage.getItem(TKEY) || "";
  function setToken(t, remember) {
    sessionStorage.removeItem(TKEY); localStorage.removeItem(TKEY);
    if (t) (remember ? localStorage : sessionStorage).setItem(TKEY, t);
  }

  /* ---------- GitHub API (uses XHR so leftover preview code can't block it) ---------- */
  function gh(method, url, body) {
    return new Promise((res, rej) => {
      const x = new XMLHttpRequest();
      x.open(method, "https://api.github.com" + url);
      x.setRequestHeader("Accept", "application/vnd.github+json");
      x.setRequestHeader("Authorization", "Bearer " + getToken());
      x.setRequestHeader("X-GitHub-Api-Version", "2022-11-28");
      if (body) x.setRequestHeader("Content-Type", "application/json");
      x.onload = () => { let j = null; try { j = JSON.parse(x.responseText); } catch (e) {} res({ status: x.status, json: j }); };
      x.onerror = () => rej(new Error("Network error. Check your internet."));
      x.send(body ? JSON.stringify(body) : null);
    });
  }
  const repoUrl = () => `/repos/${encodeURIComponent(C.owner)}/${encodeURIComponent(C.repo)}`;
  const fileUrl = p => `${repoUrl()}/contents/${p.split("/").map(encodeURIComponent).join("/")}`;
  function errText(s) {
    if (s === 401) return "Token is wrong or expired. Make a new one.";
    if (s === 403) return "Token can't write to this repo. Check Contents: Read and write.";
    if (s === 404) return "Repo, branch or file not found. Check the spelling.";
    if (s === 409 || s === 422) return "The file changed on GitHub at the same moment. Try again.";
    return "GitHub said error " + s + ".";
  }
  function toB64(str) {
    const bytes = new TextEncoder().encode(str);
    let bin = "";
    for (let i = 0; i < bytes.length; i += 0x8000) bin += String.fromCharCode.apply(null, bytes.subarray(i, i + 0x8000));
    return btoa(bin);
  }
  function fromB64(b) {
    try { return new TextDecoder().decode(Uint8Array.from(atob(b.replace(/\s/g, "")), c => c.charCodeAt(0))); }
    catch (e) { return ""; }
  }
  async function getSha(path) {
    const r = await gh("GET", fileUrl(path) + "?ref=" + encodeURIComponent(C.branch) + "&t=" + Date.now());
    if (r.status === 200) return { sha: r.json.sha, text: r.json.content ? fromB64(r.json.content) : "" };
    if (r.status === 404) return { sha: null, text: "" };
    throw new Error(errText(r.status));
  }
  async function putB64(path, b64, msg) {
    for (let tryNo = 0; tryNo < 2; tryNo++) {
      const cur = await getSha(path);
      const body = { message: msg, content: b64, branch: C.branch };
      if (cur.sha) body.sha = cur.sha;
      const r = await gh("PUT", fileUrl(path), body);
      if (r.status === 200 || r.status === 201) return;
      if ((r.status === 409 || r.status === 422) && tryNo === 0) continue;
      throw new Error(errText(r.status));
    }
  }
  const putFile = (path, text, msg) => putB64(path, toB64(text), msg);

  /* ---------- Build the file ---------- */
  const MARKS = ["real" + "Parent", "__cowork" + "HtmlReviewGuest", "window." + "claude"];
  /* Never remove the main app, the data, or our add-ons */
  const MAIN_SIG = "const PUBLIC" + "_BUILD", DATA_SIG = "const DA" + "TA", SET_SIG = "<script id=" + '"settings"';
  const isOurs = b => b.includes(MAIN_SIG) || b.startsWith(SET_SIG) || /^<script id="ppz-addon-/.test(b);
  function cleanHTML(html) {
    return html
      .replace(/<script\b[^>]*>[\s\S]*?<\/script>/gi, b =>
        !isOurs(b) && (MARKS.some(m => b.includes(m)) || b.includes("chrome-" + "extension://")) ? "" : b)
      .replace(/<style\b[^>]*>[\s\S]*?<\/style>/gi, b =>
        ["claude-" + "scrollbar", "__cwhr" + "-sel", "chrome-" + "extension://", "moz-" + "extension://"].some(m => b.includes(m)) ? "" : b);
  }
  /* Refuse to upload a broken file */
  function checkHTML(html) {
    const miss = [[MAIN_SIG, "main app code"], [DATA_SIG, "your data"], [SET_SIG, "settings block"]]
      .filter(([sig]) => !html.includes(sig)).map(([, name]) => name);
    if (miss.length) throw new Error("Stopped: the file is missing " + miss.join(", ") + ". Nothing was uploaded.");
  }
  const addonIds = html => [...html.matchAll(/<script id="(ppz-addon-[\w-]+)"/g)].map(m => m[1]);
  function sourceView(html) {
    const e = html.replace(/&/g, "&amp;").replace(/</g, "&lt;").replace(/>/g, "&gt;");
    return '<!DOCTYPE html><html><head><meta charset="UTF-8"><title>source</title></head><body><pre>' + e + "</pre></body></html>";
  }

  /* ---------- Publish ---------- */
  let busy = false;
  async function publish() {
    if (busy) return;
    const token = getToken();
    if (!token) { toast("Add your GitHub token first"); openConn(); return; }
    if (!confirm("Publish your edits to the live website?\n\nThis replaces " + C.path + " on GitHub with what's in this editor.")) return;
    busy = true; setLog("⏳ Building file…");
    try {
      const html = cleanHTML(buildHTML(false));
      if (html.includes(token)) throw new Error("Stopped: your token ended up inside the page text. Remove it and try again.");
      checkHTML(html);
      setLog("⏳ Checking GitHub…");
      const remote = await getSha(C.path);
      if (remote.text) {
        const mine = addonIds(html), missing = addonIds(remote.text).filter(id => !mine.includes(id));
        if (missing.length && !confirm("GitHub's file has add-ons this page doesn't:\n" + missing.join("\n") +
          "\n\nPublishing now would REMOVE them. Press Cancel, reload the live site, then publish.\n\nPublish anyway?")) {
          setLog("Cancelled. Reload the live site and try again."); busy = false; return;
        }
      }
      if (remote.text && html.length < remote.text.length * 0.6 &&
          !confirm("The new file is MUCH smaller than the one on GitHub (" + Math.round(html.length / 1024) + " KB vs " +
            Math.round(remote.text.length / 1024) + " KB). Something might be missing.\n\nPublish anyway?")) {
        setLog("Cancelled. Nothing was uploaded."); busy = false; return;
      }
      const msg = "Update from ProxyPathz editor (" + new Date().toLocaleString() + ")";
      setLog("⏳ Uploading " + C.path + "…");
      await putFile(C.path, html, msg);
      if (C.sv) { setLog("⏳ Uploading source-view.html…"); await putFile("source-view.html", sourceView(html), msg); }
      C.last = Date.now(); saveC();
      setLog("✅ Published! The live site updates in about a minute.");
      toast("🚀 Published to GitHub");
    } catch (e) {
      setLog("❌ " + e.message);
    }
    busy = false;
  }

  /* ---------- UI in the developer panel ---------- */
  const escA = s => String(s).replace(/&/g, "&amp;").replace(/"/g, "&quot;").replace(/</g, "&lt;");
  function setLog(t) { const el = $("ppzPubLog"); if (el) el.textContent = t; }
  function openConn() { const d = $("ppzPubConn"); if (d) d.open = true; }
  const lastText = () => C.last ? "Last published: " + new Date(C.last).toLocaleString() : "Not published from this device yet.";

  function addPublish() {
    const body = $("devBody");
    if (!body || !body.querySelector('[data-act="discard"]') || body.querySelector("#ppzPub")) return;
    const has = !!getToken();
    const sec = document.createElement("div");
    sec.id = "ppzPub";
    sec.style.cssText = "border:1px solid var(--accent);border-radius:12px;padding:12px;margin-bottom:16px;background:var(--panel2)";
    sec.innerHTML = `
      <h3 style="margin-top:0">🚀 Publish to GitHub</h3>
      <p class="sub">Puts your edits on the real website for everyone. ${lastText()}</p>
      <button type="button" class="btn full" id="ppzPubGo"
        style="background:var(--accent);color:#12081f;font-weight:700;min-height:48px">🚀 Publish now</button>
      <p class="sub" id="ppzPubLog" style="margin-top:8px">${has ? "Ready." : "Connect GitHub below first."}</p>
      <details id="ppzUpBox" style="margin-top:8px">
        <summary style="cursor:pointer;padding:8px 0">📁 Upload a file to GitHub</summary>
        <p class="sub">Pick a file from your device, like an updated ProxyPathz.html or a picture. To replace the live site, pick the HTML file and keep the name below as index.html.</p>
        <input type="file" id="ppzUpFile" style="margin-bottom:8px;width:100%">
        <label class="field"><span>Save it on GitHub as</span><input id="ppzUpPath" value="${escA(C.path)}" placeholder="index.html or images/photo.png"></label>
        <button type="button" class="btn full" id="ppzUpGo">⬆ Upload file</button>
      </details>
      <details id="ppzPubConn" ${has ? "" : "open"} style="margin-top:8px">
        <summary style="cursor:pointer;padding:8px 0">⚙ GitHub connection ${has ? "✅" : "(not connected)"}</summary>
        <label class="field"><span>Token (starts with github_pat_)</span>
          <input type="password" id="ppzPubTok" autocomplete="off" placeholder="${has ? "Saved. Paste a new one to replace it" : "Paste token here"}"></label>
        <label style="display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:center;margin:6px 0 10px">
          <input type="checkbox" id="ppzPubRem" ${C.remember ? "checked" : ""} style="width:22px;height:22px">
          <span>Remember on this device <small style="color:var(--muted)">(only on your own device)</small></span></label>
        <div class="row">
          <label class="field" style="flex:1"><span>Username</span><input id="ppzPubOwner" value="${escA(C.owner)}"></label>
          <label class="field" style="flex:1"><span>Repo</span><input id="ppzPubRepo" value="${escA(C.repo)}"></label>
        </div>
        <div class="row">
          <label class="field" style="flex:1"><span>Branch</span><input id="ppzPubBranch" value="${escA(C.branch)}"></label>
          <label class="field" style="flex:1"><span>File</span><input id="ppzPubPath" value="${escA(C.path)}"></label>
        </div>
        <label style="display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:center;margin:6px 0 10px">
          <input type="checkbox" id="ppzPubSv" ${C.sv ? "checked" : ""} style="width:22px;height:22px">
          <span>Also update source-view.html</span></label>
        <div class="row">
          <button type="button" class="btn sm" id="ppzPubSave">💾 Save</button>
          <button type="button" class="btn sm" id="ppzPubTest">🔌 Test</button>
          <button type="button" class="btn sm danger" id="ppzPubForget">Forget token</button>
        </div>
      </details>`;
    body.prepend(sec);

    const readForm = () => {
      C.owner = $("ppzPubOwner").value.trim(); C.repo = $("ppzPubRepo").value.trim();
      C.branch = $("ppzPubBranch").value.trim() || "main"; C.path = $("ppzPubPath").value.trim() || "index.html";
      C.sv = $("ppzPubSv").checked; C.remember = $("ppzPubRem").checked;
      const t = $("ppzPubTok").value.trim();
      setToken(t || getToken(), C.remember);
      $("ppzPubTok").value = "";
      saveC();
    };
    $("ppzPubGo").onclick = publish;
    $("ppzUpFile").onchange = () => {
      const f = $("ppzUpFile").files[0]; if (!f) return;
      $("ppzUpPath").value = /\.html?$/i.test(f.name) ? C.path : "images/" + f.name.replace(/[^\w.\-]/g, "_");
    };
    $("ppzUpGo").onclick = async () => {
      const f = $("ppzUpFile").files[0], path = $("ppzUpPath").value.trim().replace(/^\/+/, "");
      if (!getToken()) { toast("Add your GitHub token first"); openConn(); return; }
      if (!f || !path) return setLog("Choose a file first.");
      if (f.size > 25 * 1024 * 1024) return setLog("That file is over 25 MB, too big to upload this way.");
      if (!confirm("Upload " + f.name + " to " + C.owner + "/" + C.repo + " as " + path + "?\n\nIf that file already exists it will be replaced.")) return;
      setLog("⏳ Reading file…");
      try {
        const b64 = await new Promise((res, rej) => {
          const r = new FileReader(); r.onerror = rej;
          r.onload = () => res(String(r.result).split(",")[1] || ""); r.readAsDataURL(f);
        });
        setLog("⏳ Uploading " + path + "…");
        await putB64(path, b64, "Upload " + path + " from ProxyPathz editor");
        setLog("✅ Uploaded " + path + ". The live site updates in about a minute.");
        toast("🚀 File uploaded");
      } catch (e) { setLog("❌ " + e.message); }
    };
    $("ppzPubSave").onclick = () => { readForm(); toast("Connection saved"); sec.remove(); addPublish(); };
    $("ppzPubTest").onclick = async () => {
      readForm();
      if (!getToken()) return setLog("Paste your token first.");
      setLog("⏳ Testing…");
      try { await getSha(C.path); setLog("✅ Connected! Found " + C.path + " in " + C.owner + "/" + C.repo + "."); }
      catch (e) { setLog("❌ " + e.message); }
    };
    $("ppzPubForget").onclick = () => {
      if (!confirm("Forget the GitHub token on this device?")) return;
      setToken("", false); toast("Token forgotten"); sec.remove(); addPublish();
    };
  }

  const baseDev = renderDev;
  renderDev = function () { baseDev.apply(this, arguments); addPublish(); };
  if ($("dev").classList.contains("open")) renderDev();
})();
</script>
<script id="ppz-addon-pageedit">
/* ===== ProxyPathz add-on: Page Editor (move, hide, edit text, add shapes anywhere) ===== */
(() => {
  if (window.__ppzPE) return; window.__ppzPE = true;
  const FONTS = [["", "Default"], ["Poppins", "Poppins"], ["Orbitron", "Orbitron"], ["Russo One", "Russo One"],
    ["Space Grotesk", "Space Grotesk"], ["JetBrains Mono", "JetBrains Mono"]];
  const NAMES = { rect: "▭ Rectangle", circle: "◯ Circle", text: "🔤 Text", img: "🖼 Picture", btn: "🔗 Link button" };
  const SKIP = new Set(["images", "canvas", "guest", "devHash", "_build", "pageLayout", "pageOrder", "pageText", "overlay", "bg"]);
  const DEV = typeof PUBLIC_BUILD === "undefined" || !PUBLIC_BUILD;
  const norm = s => String(s || "").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
  const clamp = (v, a, b) => Math.max(a, Math.min(b, v));
  const r1 = v => Math.round(v * 10) / 10;
  const S = () => D.site;
  const lay = () => S().pageLayout || {};
  const layW = () => (S().pageLayout ||= {});
  const shapes = () => Array.isArray(S().overlay) ? S().overlay : [];
  const shapesW = () => Array.isArray(S().overlay) ? S().overlay : (S().overlay = []);
  const safeHref = h => { const u = String(h || "").trim(); return !u || /^\s*(javascript|data|vbscript):/i.test(u) ? "#" : u; };

  /* ---------- Styles ---------- */
  document.getElementById("ppzPECss")?.remove();
  const css = document.createElement("style");
  css.id = "ppzPECss"; css.setAttribute("data-ppzv", "");
  css.textContent = `
.ppz-hid { display:none !important; }
.ppz-anc { position:relative; isolation:isolate; }
.ppz-ov { position:absolute; box-sizing:border-box; display:flex; align-items:center; justify-content:center; text-align:center;
  overflow:hidden; background-position:center; background-repeat:no-repeat; line-height:1.2; padding:2px; word-break:break-word; }
.ppz-ov.ppz-btn { text-decoration:none; font-weight:600; cursor:pointer; box-shadow:0 4px 14px rgba(0,0,0,.35); }
.ppz-ov.ppz-text { background:transparent; text-shadow:0 1px 3px rgba(0,0,0,.5); }
body.ppz-pe { padding-top:64px; padding-bottom:46vh; }
body.ppz-pe [data-ppzk] { outline:1px dashed rgba(166,107,255,.45); outline-offset:4px; }
body.ppz-pe .ppz-selpage { outline:2px solid #4dd2ff !important; outline-offset:2px; touch-action:none; cursor:move; }
body.ppz-pe .ppz-hidE { opacity:.35; outline:2px dashed #ff5c7a !important; }
body.ppz-pe .ppz-ov { outline:1px dashed rgba(255,255,255,.55); cursor:move; }
body.ppz-pe .ppz-ov.ppz-sel { outline:2px solid #4dd2ff; touch-action:none; overflow:visible; }
body.ppz-pe [contenteditable] { outline:2px solid #ffd84d !important; background:rgba(255,216,77,.08); cursor:text; }
.ppz-rs { position:absolute; right:-4px; bottom:-4px; width:28px; height:28px; background:#4dd2ff; border-radius:8px;
  touch-action:none; cursor:nwse-resize; }
@media (pointer:coarse) { .ppz-rs { width:38px; height:38px; } }
body.ppz-pe #ppzGear, body.ppz-pe #ppzSet, body.ppz-pe #fab, body.ppz-pe #ppzPEbtn { display:none !important; }
#ppzPEui[hidden], #ppzPEbtn[hidden] { display:none !important; }
#ppzPEbar { position:fixed; top:0; left:0; right:0; z-index:70; display:flex; flex-wrap:wrap; gap:6px; align-items:center;
  padding:8px 10px; background:rgba(18,8,31,.96); border-bottom:1px solid var(--accent); }

  background:var(--panel2); color:var(--text); font:inherit; font-size:.9rem; cursor:pointer; touch-action:manipulation; }
#ppzPEui button.pri { background:var(--accent); border-color:var(--accent); color:#12081f; font-weight:700; }
#ppzPEui button.bad { border-color:var(--bad); color:var(--bad); }
