  /* ---------- Title bar: works out the final look for every card ---------- */
  function barFor(p) {
    const S = D.site || {};
    const mine = !!V.cap;
    let s = mine ? V.cap : ((p && p.capStyle) || S.capStyle || "default");
    s = OLD[s] || s;
    let c1 = mine ? V.c1 : ((p && p.capC1) || S.capC1 || "#a66bff");
    let c2 = mine ? V.c2 : ((p && p.capC2) || S.capC2 || "#2b6bff");
    if (!HEX.test(c1)) c1 = "#a66bff"; if (!HEX.test(c2)) c2 = "#2b6bff";
    let vars = `--c1:${c1};--c2:${c2};`, extra = "";
    if (s === "drawn") {
      const src = mine ? getDraw() : (S.capDrawn || "");
      if (src) vars += `--cd:url("${src}");`; else s = "frosted";
    }
    if (s === "picture") {
      const src = typeof img === "function" ? img((p && p.capImage) || S.capImage || "") : "";
      if (src) vars += `--ci:url("${src}");`; else s = "frosted";
    }
    if (DARK.has(s)) extra = " cb-dark";
    else if (s === "solid" || s === "duo" || s === "duoflow") extra = lum(c1) > .62 ? " cb-dark" : " cb-light";
    else if (!mine) { const t = (p && p.capText) || S.capText; if (t === "dark") extra = " cb-dark"; if (t === "light") extra = " cb-light"; }
    if (s === "default") return null;
    return `<div class="info cb-${s}${extra}" style="${esc(vars)}">`;
  }
  if (typeof tile === "function") {
    const baseTile = tile;
    tile = function (p) {
      const h = baseTile.apply(this, arguments);
      const open = barFor(p);
      return h.replace(/<div class="info[^"]*"(?: style="[^"]*")?>/, open || '<div class="info">');
    };
  }

  /* ---------- Title effects ---------- */
  new MutationObserver(() => { if (document.documentElement.hasAttribute("data-vtitle")) document.documentElement.removeAttribute("data-vtitle"); })
    .observe(document.documentElement, { attributes: true, attributeFilter: ["data-vtitle"] });
  document.documentElement.removeAttribute("data-vtitle");
  function applyTE() {
    const t = $("siteTitle"); if (!t) return;
    [...t.classList].filter(c => c.startsWith("ppz-te-")).forEach(c => t.classList.remove(c));
    const te = TES.some(x => x[0] === V.te) ? V.te : "";
    if (te) t.classList.add("ppz-te-" + te);
    if (te === "glitch") t.setAttribute("data-text", t.textContent); else t.removeAttribute("data-text");
  }

  /* ---------- Hover fix: drop the highlight after tapping a card ---------- */
  document.addEventListener("click", e => { const t = e.target.closest && e.target.closest(".tile"); if (t) setTimeout(() => t.blur(), 0); });

  /* ---------- Settings: Title bar tab ---------- */
  const panel = $("ppzSet");
  function mini(key) {
    let open;
    if (key === "") open = barFor(null) || '<div class="info">';
    else {
      const keep = { ...V }; V.cap = key;
      open = key === "drawn" && !getDraw() ? '<div class="info cb-frosted">' : (barFor(null) || '<div class="info">');
      Object.assign(V, keep);
    }
    return `<div class="tile ppzMini">${open}<h3>Space</h3><small>click to see more</small></div></div>`;
  }
  function addBarSection() {
    if (!panel || !panel.lastElementChild || panel.querySelector("#ppzBarSec")) return;
    const sec = document.createElement("div");
    sec.id = "ppzBarSec";
    const cur = V.cap || "";
    sec.innerHTML = `<h3 style="margin-top:12px">🏷 Card title bar</h3>
      <div class="ppzBarGrid">${BARS.map(([k, l]) => `<button type="button" data-bar="${k}" class="${k === cur ? "on" : ""}">
        ${mini(k)}<span class="lbl">${l}</span></button>`).join("")}</div>
      ${USES_COLORS.has(cur) ? `<label><span>Your colors</span><span style="display:flex;gap:6px">
        <input type="color" data-bc="c1" value="${V.c1}" style="width:44px;height:36px"><input type="color" data-bc="c2" value="${V.c2}" style="width:44px;height:36px"></span></label>` : ""}
      ${cur === "drawn" ? `<div class="ppzDrawMsg">🎨 <b>Make it yours!</b> You can draw your own custom title bar by tapping <b>✏️ Open editor</b>.
        Download your drawing to keep it or use it on another device.
        <div class="row" style="margin-top:8px"><button type="button" data-d2="open">✏️ Open editor</button>
        ${getDraw() ? '<button type="button" data-d2="dl">⬇ Download</button>' : ""}</div></div>` : ""}
      <h3 style="margin-top:14px">✨ Title effect</h3>
      <label><span>Effect on the big title</span><select data-te>${TES.map(([k, l]) => `<option value="${k}" ${k === (V.te || "") ? "selected" : ""}>${l}</option>`).join("")}</select></label>
      ${unlocked() ? `<div class="row" style="margin-top:10px"><button type="button" data-d2="everyone">🌍 Use my title bar for everyone</button></div>` : ""}`;
    panel.insertBefore(sec, panel.lastElementChild);
    const redraw = () => setTimeout(() => { sec.remove(); addBarSection(); }, 0);
    sec.addEventListener("click", e => {
      const b = e.target.closest("[data-bar]");
      if (b) {
        V.cap = b.dataset.bar; saveV(); renderPage(); redraw();
        if (V.cap === "drawn") { toast("🎨 You can draw your own! Tap ✏️ Open editor"); if (!getDraw()) setTimeout(openEditor, 150); }
        return;
      }
      const d = e.target.closest("[data-d2]"); if (!d) return;
      if (d.dataset.d2 === "open") openEditor();
      if (d.dataset.d2 === "dl") downloadURL(getDraw());
      if (d.dataset.d2 === "everyone") {
        if (!V.cap) return toast("Pick a style first");
        if (V.cap === "drawn") { if (!getDraw()) return toast("Draw something first"); D.site.capDrawn = getDraw(); }
        D.site.capStyle = V.cap; D.site.capC1 = V.c1; D.site.capC2 = V.c2;
        save(); renderPage(); toast("🌍 Set for everyone. Publish to make it live");
      }
    });
    sec.addEventListener("input", e => { const k = e.target.dataset.bc; if (k) { V[k] = e.target.value; saveV(); renderPage(); } });
    sec.addEventListener("change", e => { if (e.target.hasAttribute("data-te")) { V.te = e.target.value; saveV(); applyTE(); } });
  }
  if (panel) { new MutationObserver(addBarSection).observe(panel, { childList: true }); addBarSection(); }
  const hideOld = document.createElement("style");
  hideOld.setAttribute("data-ppzv", "");
  hideOld.textContent = `#ppzCosSec label:has(select[data-c="cap"]), #ppzCosSec label:has(input[data-c="c1"]),
    #ppzCosSec label:has(select[data-c="title"]) { display:none !important; }`;
  document.head.appendChild(hideOld);

  /* ---------- Drawing editor ---------- */
  function downloadURL(url, name) {
    if (!url) return;
    const a = document.createElement("a"); a.href = url; a.download = name || "ProxyPathz-title-bar.png";
    document.body.appendChild(a); a.click(); a.remove();
  }
  let ed = null, cv, ctx, tool = "brush", drawing = null, hist = [];
  function openEditor() {
    if (!ed) buildEditor();
    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, cv.width, cv.height); hist = [];
    const src = getDraw() || (D.site && D.site.capDrawn) || "";
    if (src) { const im = new Image(); im.onload = () => { ctx.drawImage(im, 0, 0, cv.width, cv.height); updPrev(); }; im.src = src; }
    else starter("purple");
    ed.hidden = false;
    if (panel) panel.hidden = true;
  }
  function buildEditor() {
    ed = document.createElement("div");
    ed.id = "ppzDraw"; ed.hidden = true; ed.setAttribute("data-ppzv", "");
    ed.innerHTML = `<div class="pd-box">
      <div class="pd-head"><b>🎨 Title bar editor</b><button data-d="close" aria-label="Close">✕</button></div>
      <div class="pd-prev"><div class="tile ppzMini"><div class="info cb-drawn" id="pdPrev"><h3>Space</h3><small>click to see more</small></div></div><span>Live preview</span></div>
      <canvas id="pdCv" width="600" height="150"></canvas>
      <div class="pd-row">
        <button data-tool="brush" class="on">✏️ Brush</button><button data-tool="eraser">🧽 Eraser</button>
        <button data-tool="line">📏 Line</button><button data-tool="rect">▭ Box</button><button data-tool="circle">⬤ Circle</button>
        <button data-tool="text">🔤 Text</button><button data-tool="fill">🪣 Fill</button></div>
      <div class="pd-row"><label>Color <input type="color" id="pdColor" value="#a66bff"></label>
        <label>Size <input type="range" id="pdSize" min="1" max="40" value="8"></label>
        <button data-d="undo">↶ Undo</button><button data-d="clear">🗑 Clear</button></div>
      <div class="pd-row"><span style="font-size:.8rem;color:var(--muted)">Start from:</span>
        <button data-st="purple">💜 Purple</button><button data-st="sunset">🌅 Sunset</button><button data-st="ocean">🌊 Ocean</button>
        <button data-st="grid">🟪 Grid</button><button data-st="stars">✨ Stars</button></div>
      <div class="pd-row"><button data-d="load">🖼 Load picture</button><button data-d="download">⬇ Download</button>
        <button data-d="save" class="pri">✅ Save &amp; use</button>
        ${unlocked() ? '<button data-d="everyone">🌍 Use for everyone</button>' : ""}</div></div>`;
    document.body.appendChild(ed);
    cv = ed.querySelector("#pdCv"); ctx = cv.getContext("2d");
    const color = () => ed.querySelector("#pdColor").value, size = () => +ed.querySelector("#pdSize").value;
    const pos = e => { const r = cv.getBoundingClientRect(); return [(e.clientX - r.left) * cv.width / r.width, (e.clientY - r.top) * cv.height / r.height]; };
    const snap = () => { hist.push(ctx.getImageData(0, 0, cv.width, cv.height)); if (hist.length > 30) hist.shift(); };
    cv.addEventListener("pointerdown", e => {
      e.preventDefault(); try { cv.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId); } catch (err) {}
      const [x, y] = pos(e); snap();
      ctx.globalCompositeOperation = "source-over"; ctx.fillStyle = ctx.strokeStyle = color();
      if (tool === "fill") { ctx.globalCompositeOperation = "destination-over"; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, cv.width, cv.height); ctx.globalCompositeOperation = "source-over"; updPrev(); return; }
      if (tool === "text") {
        const t = prompt("Text to add:"); if (!t) { hist.pop(); return; }
        ctx.font = `bold ${size() * 3 + 10}px Poppins, sans-serif`; ctx.textAlign = "center"; ctx.textBaseline = "middle";
        ctx.fillText(t, x, y); updPrev(); return;
      }
      if (tool === "brush" || tool === "eraser") {
        if (tool === "eraser") ctx.globalCompositeOperation = "destination-out";
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x, y, size() / 2, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();
        drawing = { x, y, id: e.pointerId };
      } else drawing = { x0: x, y0: y, id: e.pointerId, base: ctx.getImageData(0, 0, cv.width, cv.height) };
    });
    cv.addEventListener("pointermove", e => {
      if (!drawing || e.pointerId !== drawing.id) return;
      const [x, y] = pos(e);
      if (tool === "brush" || tool === "eraser") {
        ctx.lineWidth = size(); ctx.lineCap = ctx.lineJoin = "round";
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(drawing.x, drawing.y); ctx.lineTo(x, y); ctx.stroke();
        drawing.x = x; drawing.y = y; return;
      }
      ctx.putImageData(drawing.base, 0, 0);
      ctx.fillStyle = ctx.strokeStyle = color();
      if (tool === "line") { ctx.lineWidth = size(); ctx.lineCap = "round"; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(drawing.x0, drawing.y0); ctx.lineTo(x, y); ctx.stroke(); }
      if (tool === "rect") ctx.fillRect(Math.min(drawing.x0, x), Math.min(drawing.y0, y), Math.abs(x - drawing.x0), Math.abs(y - drawing.y0));
      if (tool === "circle") {
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.ellipse((drawing.x0 + x) / 2, (drawing.y0 + y) / 2, Math.abs(x - drawing.x0) / 2 || 1, Math.abs(y - drawing.y0) / 2 || 1, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
      }
    });
    const end = e => { if (!drawing || e.pointerId !== drawing.id) return; drawing = null; ctx.globalCompositeOperation = "source-over"; updPrev(); };
    cv.addEventListener("pointerup", end); cv.addEventListener("pointercancel", end);
    ed.addEventListener("click", e => {
      e.stopPropagation();
      if (e.target === ed) { ed.hidden = true; return; }
      const t = e.target.closest("[data-tool]");
      if (t) { tool = t.dataset.tool; ed.querySelectorAll("[data-tool]").forEach(b => b.classList.toggle("on", b === t)); return; }
      const st = e.target.closest("[data-st]"); if (st) { snap(); starter(st.dataset.st); return; }
      const d = e.target.closest("[data-d]"); if (!d) return;
      const a = d.dataset.d;
      if (a === "close") ed.hidden = true;
      if (a === "undo") { const h = hist.pop(); if (h) { ctx.putImageData(h, 0, 0); updPrev(); } else toast("Nothing to undo"); }
      if (a === "clear") { snap(); ctx.clearRect(0, 0, cv.width, cv.height); updPrev(); }
      if (a === "download") cv.toBlob(b => { const u = URL.createObjectURL(b); downloadURL(u); setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(u), 4000); });
      if (a === "load") {
        const inp = document.createElement("input"); inp.type = "file"; inp.accept = "image/*";
        inp.onchange = () => {
          const f = inp.files[0]; if (!f) return;
          const r = new FileReader();
          r.onload = () => { const im = new Image(); im.onload = () => {
            snap(); const k = Math.max(cv.width / im.width, cv.height / im.height), w = im.width * k, h = im.height * k;
            ctx.clearRect(0, 0, cv.width, cv.height); ctx.drawImage(im, (cv.width - w) / 2, (cv.height - h) / 2, w, h); updPrev();
          }; im.src = r.result; };
          r.readAsDataURL(f);
        };
        inp.click();
      }
      if (a === "save" || a === "everyone") {
        let url = cv.toDataURL("image/png");
        if (url.length > 450000) url = cv.toDataURL("image/webp", .85);
        if (a === "save") {
          try { localStorage.setItem(DKEY, url); } catch (err) { return toast("Too big to save on this device. Try fewer details."); }
          V.cap = "drawn"; saveV(); toast("✅ Saved! Your cards use your drawing now");
        } else {
          D.site.capDrawn = url; D.site.capStyle = "drawn"; save();
          toast("🌍 Set for everyone. Publish to make it live");
        }
        renderPage(); ed.hidden = true;
        const s = panel && panel.querySelector("#ppzBarSec"); if (s) { s.remove(); addBarSection(); }
      }
    });
  }
  function starter(k) {
    const w = cv.width, h = cv.height;
    ctx.globalCompositeOperation = "source-over"; ctx.clearRect(0, 0, w, h);
    const g = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, w, h);
    const sets = { purple: ["#2a1059", "#a66bff"], sunset: ["#ff7a59", "#a33dff"], ocean: ["#003a66", "#00e0d0"], grid: ["#12081f", "#1d0f33"], stars: ["#05030c", "#1a0f33"] };
    const [a, b] = sets[k] || sets.purple;
    g.addColorStop(0, a); g.addColorStop(1, b); ctx.fillStyle = g; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, w, h);
    if (k === "grid") { ctx.strokeStyle = "rgba(166,107,255,.35)"; ctx.lineWidth = 1;
      for (let x = 0; x < w; x += 25) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x, 0); ctx.lineTo(x, h); ctx.stroke(); }
      for (let y = 0; y < h; y += 25) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(0, y); ctx.lineTo(w, y); ctx.stroke(); } }
    if (k === "stars") for (let i = 0; i < 90; i++) { ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(255,255,255,${Math.random() * .8 + .2})`;
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(Math.random() * w, Math.random() * h, Math.random() * 1.6 + .3, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill(); }
    updPrev();
  }
  function updPrev() { const p = ed && ed.querySelector("#pdPrev"); if (p) p.style.setProperty("--cd", `url("${cv.toDataURL("image/png")}")`); }

  /* ---------- Top category bar + arrange ---------- */
  const loadA = () => { try { return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(AKEY)) || null; } catch (e) { return null; } };
  const saveA = a => { try { a ? localStorage.setItem(AKEY, JSON.stringify(a)) : localStorage.removeItem(AKEY); } catch (e) {} };
  const arrangement = () => loadA() || { order: D.site.catOrder || [], hide: D.site.catHide || [] };
  const chipsEl = () => $("ppzChips");
  const wrap = document.querySelector(".wrap") || document.body;
  const bar = document.createElement("nav");
  bar.id = "ppzCatBar"; bar.hidden = true; bar.setAttribute("data-ppzv", ""); bar.setAttribute("aria-label", "Categories");
  wrap.prepend(bar);
  let barKey = "";
  function applyArrange() {
    const src = chipsEl(); if (!src) return;
    const A = arrangement(), btns = [...src.querySelectorAll("[data-cat]")];
    const idx = k => k === "all" ? -1 : (A.order.indexOf(k) < 0 ? 1000 + btns.findIndex(b => b.dataset.cat === k) : A.order.indexOf(k));
    const sorted = btns.slice().sort((a, b) => idx(a.dataset.cat) - idx(b.dataset.cat));
    if (sorted.some((b, i) => b !== btns[i])) sorted.forEach(b => src.appendChild(b));
    btns.forEach(b => {
      const want = b.dataset.cat !== "all" && A.hide.includes(b.dataset.cat) && !b.classList.contains("on") ? "none" : "";
      if (b.style.display !== want) b.style.display = want;
    });
  }
  function syncBar() {
    const src = chipsEl(); if (!src) { bar.hidden = true; return; }
    const btns = [...src.querySelectorAll("[data-cat]")].filter(b => b.style.display !== "none");
    const key = btns.map(b => b.dataset.cat + (b.classList.contains("on") ? "*" : "")).join("|");
    bar.hidden = !btns.length;
    if (key === barKey) return; barKey = key;
    bar.innerHTML = btns.map(b => `<button type="button" data-cat="${esc(b.dataset.cat)}" class="${b.classList.contains("on") ? "on" : ""}">${esc(b.textContent)}</button>`).join("")
      + `<button type="button" class="arr" data-arr>⇅ Arrange</button>`;
  }
  function refreshCats() { applyArrange(); syncBar(); }
  bar.addEventListener("click", e => {
    if (e.target.closest("[data-arr]")) return openArrange();
    const b = e.target.closest("[data-cat]"); if (!b) return;
    const src = chipsEl() && chipsEl().querySelector(`[data-cat="${CSS.escape(b.dataset.cat)}"]`);
    if (src) src.click();
    const all = $("all"); if (all) all.closest(".box").scrollIntoView({ behavior: "smooth", block: "start" });
  });
  const startCats = () => {
    const src = chipsEl(); if (!src) return false;
    new MutationObserver(refreshCats).observe(src, { childList: true, subtree: true, attributes: true, attributeFilter: ["class"] });
    refreshCats(); return true;
  };
  if (!startCats()) { const t = setInterval(() => { if (startCats()) clearInterval(t); }, 500); setTimeout(() => clearInterval(t), 10000); }

  let arr = null, arrMode = "me";
  function openArrange() {
    if (!arr) {
      arr = document.createElement("div");
      arr.id = "ppzArr"; arr.setAttribute("data-ppzv", "");
      document.body.appendChild(arr);
      arr.addEventListener("click", onArr);
    }
    arrMode = "me"; drawArr(); arr.hidden = false;
  }
  function workingCopy() {
    const base = arrMode === "everyone" ? { order: D.site.catOrder || [], hide: D.site.catHide || [] } : arrangement();
    const keys = [...chipsEl().querySelectorAll("[data-cat]")].map(b => [b.dataset.cat, b.textContent]).filter(([k]) => k !== "all");
    const idx = k => base.order.indexOf(k) < 0 ? 1000 + keys.findIndex(x => x[0] === k) : base.order.indexOf(k);
    keys.sort((a, b) => idx(a[0]) - idx(b[0]));
    return { keys, hide: base.hide.slice() };
  }
  function drawArr() {
    const w = workingCopy();
    arr.innerHTML = `<div class="pd-box" style="max-width:460px">
      <div class="pd-head"><b>⇅ Arrange categories</b><button data-a="close" aria-label="Close">✕</button></div>
      ${unlocked() ? `<div class="pd-row">
        <button data-a="mode" data-m="me" class="${arrMode === "me" ? "on" : ""}">👤 Just me</button>
        <button data-a="mode" data-m="everyone" class="${arrMode === "everyone" ? "on" : ""}">🌍 Everyone</button></div>` : ""}
      <p style="font-size:.8rem;color:var(--muted);margin:4px 0 8px">${arrMode === "everyone"
        ? "Sets the default order for every visitor. Publish to make it live. Visitors can still arrange their own."
        : "Only changes your screen. Other people won't see this."}</p>
      ${w.keys.length ? w.keys.map(([k, l], i) => `<div class="pa-row ${w.hide.includes(k) ? "off" : ""}">
        <span>${esc(l)}</span>
        <button data-a="up" data-i="${i}" ${i === 0 ? "disabled" : ""} aria-label="Move up">▲</button>
        <button data-a="down" data-i="${i}" ${i === w.keys.length - 1 ? "disabled" : ""} aria-label="Move down">▼</button>
        <button data-a="eye" data-i="${i}" aria-label="Show or hide">${w.hide.includes(k) ? "🙈" : "👁"}</button>
      </div>`).join("") : '<p style="font-size:.85rem">No categories yet. Add tags to your proxies first.</p>'}
      <div class="pd-row" style="margin-top:12px">
        <button data-a="reset">↺ Reset</button>
        <button data-a="close" class="pri">Done</button></div></div>`;
  }
  function commitArr(w) {
    const data = { order: w.keys.map(k => k[0]), hide: w.hide };
    if (arrMode === "everyone") { D.site.catOrder = data.order; D.site.catHide = data.hide; save(); }
    else saveA(data);
    barKey = ""; refreshCats(); drawArr();
  }
  function onArr(e) {
    e.stopPropagation();
    if (e.target === arr) { arr.hidden = true; return; }
    const b = e.target.closest("[data-a]"); if (!b || b.disabled) return;
    const a = b.dataset.a, i = +b.dataset.i;
    if (a === "close") { arr.hidden = true; return; }
    if (a === "mode") { arrMode = b.dataset.m; drawArr(); return; }
    if (a === "reset") {
      if (arrMode === "everyone") { delete D.site.catOrder; delete D.site.catHide; save(); toast("Default order reset"); }
      else { saveA(null); toast("Back to the site's order"); }
      barKey = ""; refreshCats(); drawArr(); return;
    }
    const w = workingCopy();
    if (a === "up" && i > 0) [w.keys[i - 1], w.keys[i]] = [w.keys[i], w.keys[i - 1]];
    if (a === "down" && i < w.keys.length - 1) [w.keys[i + 1], w.keys[i]] = [w.keys[i], w.keys[i + 1]];
    if (a === "eye") {
      const k = w.keys[i][0], j = w.hide.indexOf(k);
      j >= 0 ? w.hide.splice(j, 1) : w.hide.push(k);
    }
    commitArr(w);
  }
  document.addEventListener("keydown", e => {
    if (e.key !== "Escape") return;
    if (arr && !arr.hidden) arr.hidden = true;
    if (ed && !ed.hidden) ed.hidden = true;
  });

  /* ---------- Re-apply after every page redraw ---------- */
  const basePage = renderPage;
  renderPage = function () { basePage.apply(this, arguments); applyTE(); refreshCats(); };

  /* ---------- Keep visitor-only stuff out of exported files ---------- */
  if (typeof buildHTML === "function") {
    const baseBuild = buildHTML;
    buildHTML = function () {
      const t = $("siteTitle"), cls = t ? [...t.classList].filter(c => c.startsWith("ppz-te-")) : [];
      const txt = t && t.getAttribute("data-text");
      if (t) { cls.forEach(c => t.classList.remove(c)); t.removeAttribute("data-text"); if (!t.getAttribute("class")) t.removeAttribute("class"); }
      try { return baseBuild.apply(this, arguments); }
      finally { if (t) { cls.forEach(c => t.classList.add(c)); if (txt) t.setAttribute("data-text", txt); } }
    };
  }

  applyTE();
  renderPage();
})();
</script>
<script id="ppz-addon-texteffects">
/* ===== ProxyPathz add-on: text effects for any text (visitor setting) ===== */
(() => {
  if (window.__ppzTx) return; window.__ppzTx = true;
  const KEY = "proxypathz_v15_texteffects";
  let V = { fx: "", speed: "normal", groups: [], picked: [] };
  try { V = Object.assign(V, JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(KEY)) || {}); } catch (e) {}
  if (!Array.isArray(V.groups)) V.groups = [];
  if (!Array.isArray(V.picked)) V.picked = [];
  const saveV = () => { try { localStorage.setItem(KEY, JSON.stringify(V)); } catch (e) {} };

  const EFFECTS = [["", "Off"], ["glow", "Glow"], ["rainbow", "Rainbow"], ["neon", "Neon"], ["fire", "Fire"], ["ice", "Ice"],
    ["outline", "Outline"], ["shadow3d", "3D shadow"], ["accent", "Theme color"], ["pulse", "Pulse"]];
  const SPEEDS = { slow: 1.8, normal: 1, fast: .55 };
  const GROUPS = [
    ["all", "✅ Everything", "h1, h2, h3, h4, p, li, small, a, button, label, b, strong, em, span"],
    ["title", "🏷 Big title", "#siteTitle"],
    ["headings", "📌 Section headings", ".box > h2, .box h2, header h2"],
    ["cards", "🃏 Proxy names on cards", ".tile .info h3"],
    ["text", "📝 Normal text", "header p, .box p, .box li, footer, footer p"],
    ["small", "🔹 Small text", ".tile .info small, .box small, header small"],
    ["buttons", "🔘 Buttons and links", ".btn, a.link, .box a, #ppzCatBar button, #ppzChips button"]
  ];
  const EXCL = ":not(#dev *):not(#ppzSet *):not(#ppzDraw *):not(#ppzArr *):not(#ppzPEui *):not(#ppzTxBar *):not(#ppzGear):not(input):not(select):not(textarea)";
  const FX = {
    glow: "text-shadow:0 0 6px var(--accent),0 0 14px var(--accent) !important;",
    rainbow: "-webkit-text-fill-color:currentColor !important; animation:ppzTxRain calc(4s * var(--txs,1)) linear infinite !important;",
    neon: "color:#fff !important; -webkit-text-fill-color:#fff !important; text-shadow:0 0 2px #fff,0 0 8px var(--accent),0 0 18px var(--accent) !important; animation:ppzTxFlick calc(3.2s * var(--txs,1)) infinite !important;",
    fire: "-webkit-text-fill-color:currentColor !important; animation:ppzTxFire calc(1.2s * var(--txs,1)) ease-in-out infinite alternate !important;",
    ice: "color:#d8f6ff !important; -webkit-text-fill-color:#d8f6ff !important; animation:ppzTxIce calc(2.6s * var(--txs,1)) ease-in-out infinite alternate !important;",
    outline: "color:transparent !important; -webkit-text-fill-color:transparent !important; -webkit-text-stroke:1px var(--accent) !important;",
    shadow3d: "text-shadow:1px 1px 0 var(--accent),2px 2px 0 color-mix(in srgb,var(--accent) 70%,#000),3px 3px 0 color-mix(in srgb,var(--accent) 45%,#000),4px 4px 6px rgba(0,0,0,.5) !important;",
    accent: "color:var(--accent) !important; -webkit-text-fill-color:var(--accent) !important;",
    pulse: "animation:ppzTxPulse calc(2s * var(--txs,1)) ease-in-out infinite !important;"
  };

  /* ---------- Fixed styles ---------- */
  const css = document.createElement("style");
  css.id = "ppzTxCss"; css.setAttribute("data-ppzv", "");
  css.textContent = `
@keyframes ppzTxRain { 0%,100% { color:#ff5c7a; } 20% { color:#ffd84d; } 40% { color:#3ddc84; } 60% { color:#4dd2ff; } 80% { color:#a66bff; } }
@keyframes ppzTxFlick { 0%,19%,21%,23%,55%,57%,100% { opacity:1; } 20%,22%,56% { opacity:.55; } }
@keyframes ppzTxFire { from { color:#ffd84d; text-shadow:0 0 4px #ff9a3d,0 -2px 10px #ff3d00; }
  to { color:#ff7a1a; text-shadow:0 0 8px #ff3d00,0 -4px 16px #ff9a3d; } }
@keyframes ppzTxIce { from { text-shadow:0 0 4px #7fd8ff,0 0 10px rgba(127,216,255,.4); } to { text-shadow:0 0 8px #bff0ff,0 0 18px rgba(127,216,255,.75); } }
@keyframes ppzTxPulse { 0%,100% { opacity:1; text-shadow:none; } 50% { opacity:.75; text-shadow:0 0 12px var(--accent); } }
body.ppz-txpick .ppz-txp { outline:2px dashed var(--accent) !important; outline-offset:2px; }
body.ppz-txpick { cursor:crosshair; }
#ppzTxBar { position:fixed; left:50%; bottom:16px; transform:translateX(-50%); z-index:90; display:flex; align-items:center; gap:10px;
  padding:8px 8px 8px 16px; border-radius:999px; background:var(--panel); border:1px solid var(--accent);
  box-shadow:0 6px 24px rgba(0,0,0,.5); font-size:.85rem; color:var(--text); max-width:calc(100vw - 24px); }
#ppzTxBar[hidden] { display:none; }
#ppzTxBar button { min-height:40px; padding:0 14px; border-radius:999px; border:none; background:var(--accent); color:#12081f;
  font:inherit; font-weight:700; cursor:pointer; }
#ppzTxSec .ppzTxGroups label { padding:8px 0; }`;
  document.head.appendChild(css);
  const dyn = document.createElement("style");
  dyn.id = "ppzTxDyn"; dyn.setAttribute("data-ppzv", "");
  document.head.appendChild(dyn);

  /* ---------- Build the effect CSS ---------- */
  function apply() {
    document.documentElement.style.setProperty("--txs", SPEEDS[V.speed] || 1);
    const rule = FX[V.fx];
    if (!rule) { dyn.textContent = ""; return; }
    const parts = [];
    V.groups.forEach(g => { const G = GROUPS.find(x => x[0] === g); if (G) parts.push(...G[2].split(",")); });
    parts.push(".ppz-txp");
    const sel = parts.map(s => s.trim() + EXCL);
    const off = sel.flatMap(s => ["body.noanim " + s, "body.vnoanim " + s]);
    dyn.textContent = `${sel.join(",\n")} { ${rule} }
${off.join(",\n")} { animation:none !important; }
@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) { ${sel.join(",\n")} { animation:none !important; } }`;
  }

  /* ---------- Remembering picked text ---------- */
  const kids = el => [...el.children].filter(c => !c.hasAttribute("data-ppzv"));
  function keyOf(el) {
    const path = []; let cur = el;
    while (cur && cur !== document.body && cur !== document.documentElement) {
      if (cur.id && !cur.id.startsWith("ppz")) return "i:" + cur.id + ">" + path.join(".");
      if (cur.dataset && cur.dataset.ppzk) return "k:" + cur.dataset.ppzk + ">" + path.join(".");
      const p = cur.parentElement; if (!p) return null;
      const i = kids(p).indexOf(cur); if (i < 0) return null;
      path.unshift(i); cur = p;
    }
    return null;
  }
  function byKey(k) {
    const m = String(k).match(/^([ki]):([^>]*)>(.*)$/); if (!m) return null;
    let el = m[1] === "i" ? document.getElementById(m[2]) : document.querySelector('[data-ppzk="' + CSS.escape(m[2]) + '"]');
    if (!el) return null;
    if (m[3]) for (const i of m[3].split(".").map(Number)) { el = kids(el)[i]; if (!el) return null; }
    return el;
  }
  function markPicked() {
    document.querySelectorAll(".ppz-txp").forEach(e => e.classList.remove("ppz-txp"));
    V.picked.forEach(k => { const el = byKey(k); if (el) el.classList.add("ppz-txp"); });
  }

  /* ---------- Pick mode ---------- */
  let picking = false;
  const bar = document.createElement("div");
  bar.id = "ppzTxBar"; bar.hidden = true; bar.setAttribute("data-ppzv", "");
  document.body.appendChild(bar);
  function updBar() {
    bar.innerHTML = `<span>👆 Tap text to add or remove the effect · <b>${V.picked.length}</b> picked</span><button type="button" data-txdone>✅ Done</button>`;
  }
  function startPick() {
    if (!V.fx) { V.fx = "glow"; saveV(); apply(); toast("Turned on Glow so you can see it"); }
    picking = true; document.body.classList.add("ppz-txpick");
    const p = $("ppzSet"); if (p) p.hidden = true;
    updBar(); bar.hidden = false;
  }
  function stopPick() {
    picking = false; document.body.classList.remove("ppz-txpick"); bar.hidden = true;
    toast(V.picked.length + " text" + (V.picked.length === 1 ? "" : "s") + " picked");
  }
  bar.addEventListener("click", e => { e.stopPropagation(); if (e.target.closest("[data-txdone]")) stopPick(); });
  document.addEventListener("click", e => {
    if (!picking || e.target.closest("#ppzTxBar")) return;
    e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation();
    let t = e.target; if (t && t.nodeType !== 1) t = t.parentElement;
    if (!t || t.closest("#dev, #ppzSet, #ppzDraw, #ppzArr, #ppzPEui, #ppzGear, #fab, #ppzBg")) return;
    if (!t.textContent.trim()) return toast("Tap right on some text");
    const k = keyOf(t); if (!k) return toast("Can't pick that one, try the text next to it");
    const i = V.picked.indexOf(k);
    i >= 0 ? V.picked.splice(i, 1) : (V.picked.length < 150 && V.picked.push(k));
    saveV(); markPicked(); updBar();
  }, true);
  document.addEventListener("keydown", e => { if (picking && e.key === "Escape") stopPick(); });

  /* ---------- Settings: Text effects tab ---------- */
  const panel = $("ppzSet");
  function addSection() {
    if (!panel || !panel.lastElementChild || panel.querySelector("#ppzTxSec")) return;
    const sec = document.createElement("div");
    sec.id = "ppzTxSec";
    sec.innerHTML = `<h3 style="margin-top:12px">🔤 Text effects</h3>
      <label><span>Effect</span><select data-tx="fx">${EFFECTS.map(([k, l]) => `<option value="${k}" ${k === V.fx ? "selected" : ""}>${l}</option>`).join("")}</select></label>
      <label><span>Speed<small>For moving effects</small></span><select data-tx="speed">
        ${[["slow", "Slow"], ["normal", "Normal"], ["fast", "Fast"]].map(([k, l]) => `<option value="${k}" ${k === V.speed ? "selected" : ""}>${l}</option>`).join("")}</select></label>
      <p class="note" style="margin:10px 0 0">Add it to:</p>
      <div class="ppzTxGroups">${GROUPS.map(([k, l]) => `<label><span>${l}</span><input type="checkbox" data-txg="${k}" ${V.groups.includes(k) ? "checked" : ""}></label>`).join("")}</div>
      <div class="row" style="margin-top:10px">
        <button type="button" data-tx2="pick">👆 Pick text on the page</button>
        <button type="button" data-tx2="clear" ${V.picked.length ? "" : "disabled"}>Clear picked (${V.picked.length})</button></div>
      <div class="row"><button type="button" data-tx2="reset">Reset text effects</button></div>`;
    panel.insertBefore(sec, panel.lastElementChild);
    const redraw = () => setTimeout(() => { sec.remove(); addSection(); }, 0);
    sec.addEventListener("change", e => {
      const t = e.target;
      if (t.dataset.tx) { V[t.dataset.tx] = t.value; saveV(); apply(); return; }
      if (t.dataset.txg) {
        const g = t.dataset.txg, i = V.groups.indexOf(g);
        t.checked ? (i < 0 && V.groups.push(g)) : (i >= 0 && V.groups.splice(i, 1));
        if (t.checked && !V.fx) { V.fx = "glow"; toast("Turned on Glow so you can see it"); redraw(); }
        saveV(); apply();
      }
    });
    sec.addEventListener("click", e => {
      const b = e.target.closest("[data-tx2]"); if (!b || b.disabled) return;
      const a = b.dataset.tx2;
      if (a === "pick") startPick();
      if (a === "clear") { V.picked = []; saveV(); markPicked(); redraw(); toast("Cleared picked text"); }
      if (a === "reset") {
        if (!confirm("Turn off all text effects?")) return;
        V = { fx: "", speed: "normal", groups: [], picked: [] }; saveV(); apply(); markPicked(); redraw();
      }
    });
  }
  if (panel) { new MutationObserver(addSection).observe(panel, { childList: true }); addSection(); }

  /* ---------- Re-apply after every page redraw ---------- */
  const basePage = renderPage;
  renderPage = function () { basePage.apply(this, arguments); markPicked(); };

  /* ---------- Keep it out of exported files ---------- */
  if (typeof buildHTML === "function") {
    const baseBuild = buildHTML;
    buildHTML = function () {
      const picked = [...document.querySelectorAll(".ppz-txp")];
      picked.forEach(e => { e.classList.remove("ppz-txp"); if (!e.getAttribute("class")) e.removeAttribute("class"); });
      const txs = document.documentElement.style.getPropertyValue("--txs");
      document.documentElement.style.removeProperty("--txs");
      try { return baseBuild.apply(this, arguments); }
      finally { picked.forEach(e => e.classList.add("ppz-txp")); if (txs) document.documentElement.style.setProperty("--txs", txs); }
    };
  }

  apply(); markPicked();
})();
</script>

(function(){'use strict';

function x(e){try{return document.evaluate(e,document,null,9,null).singleNodeValue}catch(_){return null}}
function vis(el){return !!(el&&el.getClientRects().length)}

function valid(d){return d&&d.items&&typeof d.items==='object'}
function load(){var a=null,b=null;try{a=JSON.parse(document.getElementById(ID).textContent)}catch(_){}try{b=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(KEY)||'null')}catch(_){}
  var p=valid(a)?a:null;if(valid(b)&&(!p||(b.savedAt||0)>(p.savedAt||0)))p=b;state=p||{savedAt:0,items:{}}}
function json(){return JSON.stringify(state).replace(/</g,'\\u003c')}
function save(){state.savedAt=Date.now();try{localStorage.setItem(KEY,JSON.stringify(state))}catch(_){}
  var t=document.getElementById(ID);if(!t){t=document.createElement('script');t.type='application/json';t.id=ID;document.body.appendChild(t)}t.textContent=json()}
function applyCSS(){var out='';Object.keys(state.items).forEach(function(s){var v=state.items[s],r=[];
  if(v.d)r.push('display:inline-block!important');
  if(v.w)r.push('width:'+v.w+'px!important','max-width:100%!important','box-sizing:border-box!important');
  if(v.h)r.push(v.m?'height:'+v.h+'px!important;object-fit:cover':'min-height:'+v.h+'px!important');
  if(v.f)r.push('font-size:'+v.f+'px!important');
  if(v.z&&v.z!==100)r.push('zoom:'+(v.z/100));
  if(r.length)out+=s+'{'+r.join(';')+'}\n'});
  var st=document.getElementById('ppz-resize-css');if(!st){st=document.createElement('style');st.id='ppz-resize-css';document.head.appendChild(st)}st.textContent=out}

function cssPath(el){var parts=[];
  while(el&&el.nodeType===1&&el!==document.body&&el!==document.documentElement){
    if(el.id&&document.querySelectorAll('#'+CSS.escape(el.id)).length===1){parts.unshift('#'+CSS.escape(el.id));break}
    var tp=el.getAttribute('data-ppz-tp');if(tp){parts.unshift('[data-ppz-tp="'+tp+'"]');break}
    var i=1,s=el;while((s=s.previousElementSibling))if(s.tagName===el.tagName)i++;
    parts.unshift(el.tagName.toLowerCase()+':nth-of-type('+i+')');el=el.parentElement}
  if(!parts.length)return null;if(!/^[#\[]/.test(parts[0]))parts.unshift('body');return parts.join(' > ')}

function resolve(L){if(!lastT||!document.contains(lastT))return null;
  var t=L.textContent.replace(/\s+/g,' '),m=t.match(/Selected:\s*([a-z][a-z0-9-]*)\s*(?:[-–—:]\s*["“']?(.*?)["”']?\s*)?$/i);if(!m)return null;
  var tag=m[1].toUpperCase(),snip=(m[2]||'').replace(/(\.\.\.|…)$/,'').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim().slice(0,25),c=[],el=lastT;
  while(el&&el!==document.body){if(el.tagName===tag)c.push(el);el=el.parentElement}
  if(!c.length)return null;
  if(snip)for(var i=0;i<c.length;i++){var tx=(c[i].innerText||c[i].textContent||'').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim();if(tx.indexOf(snip)===0)return c[i]}
  return c[0]}

function measure(){var r=sel.getBoundingClientRect(),cs=getComputedStyle(sel);
  return{w:Math.round(r.width),h:Math.round(r.height),f:Math.round(parseFloat(cs.fontSize)),inl:cs.display==='inline',m:/^(IMG|VIDEO|IFRAME|CANVAS|SVG|PICTURE)$/i.test(sel.tagName)}}
function cur(){return state.items[selKey]||(state.items[selKey]={})}
function snap(){hist.push(JSON.stringify(state.items));if(hist.length>40)hist.shift()}
function commit(){Object.keys(state.items).forEach(function(k){var v=state.items[k];if(!v.w&&!v.h&&!v.f&&(!v.z||v.z===100))delete state.items[k]});applyCSS();save();info()}
function bump(k,dir){if(!sel)return;snap();var v=cur(),m=measure(),z=(v.z||100)/100;
  if(k==='w'){v.w=Math.max(30,(v.w||Math.round(m.w/z))+dir*STEP.w);if(m.inl)v.d=1}
  if(k==='h'){v.h=Math.max(20,(v.h||Math.round(m.h/z))+dir*STEP.h);v.m=m.m?1:0;if(m.inl)v.d=1}
  if(k==='f')v.f=Math.min(120,Math.max(8,(v.f||m.f)+dir*STEP.f));
  if(k==='z')v.z=Math.min(300,Math.max(30,(v.z||100)+dir*STEP.z));
  commit()}
function resetSel(){if(sel&&state.items[selKey]){snap();delete state.items[selKey];commit()}}
function undo(){if(hist.length){state.items=JSON.parse(hist.pop());commit()}}

function info(){if(!bar)return;var el=bar.querySelector('.ppz-rs-info');
  if(!sel){el.textContent='Tap something on the page to resize it.';return}
  var v=state.items[selKey]||{};
  el.textContent='Size: W '+(v.w?v.w+'px':'auto')+' · H '+(v.h?v.h+'px'+(v.m?'':' (min)'):'auto')+' · Text '+(v.f?v.f+'px':'auto')+' · Zoom '+(v.z||100)+'% — or drag the purple dots'}
function ensureBar(L){if(bar&&document.contains(bar))return;
  bar=document.createElement('div');bar.className='ppz-rs-bar';bar.setAttribute('data-ppz-rs-ui','');
  var b=[['w',-1,'↔ −'],['w',1,'↔ +'],['h',-1,'↕ −'],['h',1,'↕ +'],['f',-1,'A−'],['f',1,'A+'],['z',-1,'🔍−'],['z',1,'🔍+']];
  bar.innerHTML=b.map(function(a){return '<button type="button" data-k="'+a[0]+'" data-d="'+a[1]+'">'+a[2]+'</button>'}).join('')+
    '<button type="button" data-r title="Reset size">↺ Size</button><button type="button" data-u title="Undo size change">↶</button><div class="ppz-rs-info"></div>';
  bar.addEventListener('click',function(e){var t=e.target.closest('button');if(!t)return;e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();
    if(t.hasAttribute('data-r'))resetSel();else if(t.hasAttribute('data-u'))undo();else bump(t.dataset.k,+t.dataset.d)});
  L.insertAdjacentElement('afterend',bar);info()}

function mkHandles(){['e','s','se'].forEach(function(d){var h=document.createElement('div');h.className='ppz-rs-h';h.dataset.d=d;h.setAttribute('data-ppz-rs-ui','');
  h.addEventListener('pointerdown',startDrag);document.body.appendChild(h);H[d]=h});

function startDrag(e){if(!sel)return;e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();snap();var m=measure(),v=cur(),z=(v.z||100)/100;

function pos(h,l,t){var s=h.offsetWidth;h.style.left=Math.max(0,Math.min(innerWidth-s,l))+'px';h.style.top=Math.max(0,Math.min(innerHeight-s,t))+'px'}
function place(){var show=on&&sel&&document.contains(sel);
  ['e','s','se'].forEach(function(d){H[d].style.display=show?'block':'none'});
  if(!on)return;
  if(show){var r=sel.getBoundingClientRect(),hw=H.e.offsetWidth/2;
    pos(H.e,r.right-hw,r.top+r.height/2-hw);pos(H.s,r.left+r.width/2-hw,r.bottom-hw);pos(H.se,r.right-hw,r.bottom-hw)}
  requestAnimationFrame(place)}

document.addEventListener('pointerdown',function(e){if(!on)return;var t=e.target;
  if(t.closest('[data-ppz-rs-ui]')||roots.some(function(r){return r&&r.contains(t)}))return;
  lastT=t;setTimeout(function(){lastLabel=''},120)},true);

function poll(){var e=editorOn();
  if(!e){if(on){on=null;sel=null;if(bar)bar.remove();bar=null}return}
  var first=!on;on=e;var L=labelEl();roots=[fixedRoot(e),L&&fixedRoot(L)];
  if(L){ensureBar(L);var t=L.textContent;if(t!==lastLabel){lastLabel=t;sel=resolve(L);selKey=sel?cssPath(sel):null;if(!selKey)sel=null;info()}}
  if(first)place()}

function patchOut(html){if(typeof html!=='string'||html.indexOf('</body>')<0)return html;
  var tag='<script type="application/json" id="'+ID+'">'+json()+'<\/script>',re=/<script type="application\/json" id="ppz-resize-data">[\s\S]*?<\/script>/;
  if(re.test(html))return html.replace(re,function(){return tag});var k=html.lastIndexOf('</body>');return html.slice(0,k)+tag+'\n'+html.slice(k)}
function wrapFns(){['buildHTML','cleanHTML'].forEach(function(n){var f=window[n];if(typeof f==='function'&&!f.__ppzRs){
  var w=function(){var parked=[];
    /* Keep editor-only resize controls out of downloaded/published copies. */
    if(n==='buildHTML')document.querySelectorAll('[data-ppz-rs-ui]').forEach(function(el){
      if(el.parentNode){parked.push({el:el,parent:el.parentNode,next:el.nextSibling});el.remove()}
    });
    var r;try{r=f.apply(this,arguments)}finally{for(var i=parked.length-1;i>=0;i--){
      var q=parked[i];if(q.parent&&q.parent.isConnected)q.parent.insertBefore(q.el,q.next&&q.next.parentNode===q.parent?q.next:null)
    }}
    return r&&typeof r.then==='function'?r.then(patchOut):patchOut(r)};w.__ppzRs=true;window[n]=w}})}

})();

function esc(s){return String(s==null?'':s).replace(/[&<>"']/g,function(c){return{'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]})}
function uid(){return 'w'+Date.now().toString(36)+Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,6)}
function fmt(s){var t=esc(s);var tok=[];function h(x){tok.push(x);return '\u0000'+(tok.length-1)+'\u0000'}
  t=t.replace(/\[([^\]]+)\]\((https?:\/\/[^\s)]+)\)/g,function(m,a,u){return h('<a href="'+u+'" target="_blank" rel="noopener">'+a+'</a>')});
  t=t.replace(/(^|\s)(https?:\/\/[^\s<]+)/g,function(m,p,u){return p+h('<a href="'+u+'" target="_blank" rel="noopener">'+u+'</a>')});
  t=t.replace(/\*\*(.+?)\*\*/g,'<b>$1</b>').replace(/\*([^*\n]+)\*/g,'<i>$1</i>').replace(/\n/g,'<br>');
  return t.replace(/\u0000(\d+)\u0000/g,function(m,i){return tok[+i]})}
function okImg(u){return /^(https?:\/\/|data:image\/)/i.test(u||'')}

   (n>1?'<div class="ppz-wl-dots">'+tour.steps.map(function(_,i){return '<i class="'+(i===tour.i?'on':'')+'"></i>'}).join('')+'</div>':'<div style="height:10px"></div>')+

       '<label>Points at</label><div class="ppz-wl-row"><button class="ppz-wl-b" data-a="pick">🎯 Pick on page</button><button class="ppz-wl-b" data-a="nopick">Middle of screen</button>'+
       '<span class="ppz-wl-hint" style="flex:1;overflow-wrap:anywhere">'+(s.target?esc(s.target):'(middle of screen)')+'</span></div><input type="hidden" data-f="target" value="'+esc(s.target||'')+'"></div>'}).join('')+
     '<div class="ppz-wl-row"><button class="ppz-wl-b pri" data-a="addstep">➕ Add step</button><button class="ppz-wl-b" data-a="preview">▶ Preview</button>'+
     '<button class="ppz-wl-b" data-a="reshow">🔁 Show again to everyone</button></div>'}
  else{b.innerHTML=work.anns.map(function(a,i){return '<div class="ppz-wl-item" data-i="'+i+'"><div class="ppz-wl-row"><b style="flex:1">'+esc(a.title||'Announcement')+(expired(a)?' (ended)':'')+'</b>'+
       '<label class="ppz-wl-row" style="margin:0;font-size:.85em"><input type="checkbox" data-f="on"'+(a.on?' checked':'')+'> Active</label><button class="ppz-wl-b dan" data-a="del">🗑</button></div>'+
       '<label>Title</label><input type="text" data-f="title" value="'+esc(a.title)+'"><label>Message</label><textarea data-f="text">'+esc(a.text)+'</textarea>'+
       '<div class="ppz-wl-row"><div style="flex:1"><label>Style</label><select data-f="style"><option value="banner"'+(a.style!=='popup'?' selected':'')+'>Banner at top</option><option value="popup"'+(a.style==='popup'?' selected':'')+'>Popup (once)</option></select></div>'+
       '<div><label>Color</label><input type="color" data-f="color" value="'+esc(a.color||'#7c3aed')+'"></div><div style="flex:1"><label>End date (optional)</label><input type="date" data-f="expires" value="'+esc(a.expires||'')+'"></div></div>'+
       '<div class="ppz-wl-row"><div style="flex:2"><label>Button link (optional)</label><input type="text" data-f="link" value="'+esc(a.link||'')+'" placeholder="https://…"></div><div style="flex:1"><label>Button text</label><input type="text" data-f="linkText" value="'+esc(a.linkText||'')+'" placeholder="Open"></div></div>'+
       imgField(a.img)+'<div class="ppz-wl-row" style="margin-top:8px"><button class="ppz-wl-b" data-a="reann">🔁 Show again to everyone</button><span class="ppz-wl-hint">'+(a.rev?'Re-shown '+a.rev+'×':'')+'</span></div></div>'}).join('')+
     '<button class="ppz-wl-b pri" data-a="addann">➕ New announcement</button>'}}
function pull(){if(!work)return;var b=modal().querySelector('.ppz-wl-body');
  var on=b.querySelector('[data-g=on]');if(on)work.tour.on=on.checked;
  var list=tab==='tour'?work.tour.steps:work.anns;
  b.querySelectorAll('.ppz-wl-item').forEach(function(it){var o=list[+it.dataset.i];if(!o)return;
    it.querySelectorAll('[data-f]').forEach(function(f){var k=f.dataset.f;if(k==='imgdata')return;o[k]=f.type==='checkbox'?f.checked:f.value.trim()});
    var d=it.querySelector('[data-f=imgdata]').value;if(!o.img&&d)o.img=d;if(o.img&&!okImg(o.img))o.img='';
    if(o.link&&!/^https?:\/\//i.test(o.link))o.link=''})}
function bodyClick(e){
  var b=e.target.closest('button');if(!b)return;
  e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();pull();
  var a=b.dataset.a,it=b.closest('[data-i]'),i=it?+it.dataset.i:-1;
  var list=tab==='tour'?work.tour.steps:work.anns;
  if(a==='addstep'){work.tour.steps.push({title:'New step',text:'',img:'',target:''});draw();return}
  if(a==='addann'){work.anns.push({id:uid(),on:true,title:'New announcement',text:'',style:'banner',color:'#7c3aed',expires:'',link:'',linkText:'Open',img:'',rev:0});draw();return}
  if(a==='del'&&i>=0){list.splice(i,1);draw();return}
  if(a==='up'&&i>0){var q=list.splice(i,1)[0];list.splice(i-1,0,q);draw();return}
  if(a==='down'&&i>=0&&i<list.length-1){var q2=list.splice(i,1)[0];list.splice(i+1,0,q2);draw();return}
  if(a==='noimg'&&i>=0){list[i].img='';draw();return}
  if(a==='pick'&&tab==='tour'&&i>=0){startPick(i);return}
  if(a==='nopick'&&tab==='tour'&&i>=0){work.tour.steps[i].target='';draw();return}
  if(a==='preview'){startTour(work.tour.steps);return}
  if(a==='reshow'&&tab==='tour'){
    work.tour.ver=Math.max(1,+work.tour.ver||1)+1;
    state=work;save();work=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(state));
    startTour(state.tour.steps);return;
  }
  if(a==='reann'&&tab==='ann'&&i>=0){work.anns[i].rev=(+work.anns[i].rev||0)+1;work.anns[i].on=true;draw();return}
}

function upload(input){
  var file=input.files&&input.files[0],item=input.closest('.ppz-wl-item');if(!file||!item)return;
  if(!/^image\//i.test(file.type)){alert('Please choose an image file.');input.value='';return}
  var max=5*1024*1024;if(file.size>max){alert('That image is too large. Choose an image under 5 MB.');input.value='';return}
  pull();var ix=+item.dataset.i,arr=tab==='tour'?work.tour.steps:work.anns;
  var fr=new FileReader();fr.onload=function(){
    var data=String(fr.result||'');
    function put(v){if(!arr[ix])return;arr[ix].img=v;draw()}
    if(file.type==='image/gif'||file.size<180*1024){put(data);return}
    var im=new Image();im.onload=function(){
      var maxSide=1200,scale=Math.min(1,maxSide/Math.max(im.naturalWidth,im.naturalHeight));
      var c=document.createElement('canvas');c.width=Math.max(1,Math.round(im.naturalWidth*scale));c.height=Math.max(1,Math.round(im.naturalHeight*scale));
      var ctx=c.getContext('2d');ctx.drawImage(im,0,0,c.width,c.height);
      var out=c.toDataURL('image/jpeg',.82);put(out);
    };im.onerror=function(){put(data)};im.src=data;
  };fr.onerror=function(){alert('Could not read that image.')};fr.readAsDataURL(file);
}

function selectorFor(el){
  if(!el||el===document.body||el===document.documentElement)return '';
  if(el.id){try{return '#'+CSS.escape(el.id)}catch(_){return '#'+el.id.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]/g,'\\$&')}}
  var parts=[],node=el;
  while(node&&node.nodeType===1&&node!==document.body&&parts.length<6){
    var tag=node.tagName.toLowerCase(),part=tag;
    if(node.classList&&node.classList.length){var cls=Array.from(node.classList).filter(function(c){return /^[a-zA-Z_-][\w-]*$/.test(c)&&!/^ppz-wl-/.test(c)}).slice(0,2);if(cls.length)part+='.'+cls.join('.')}
    if(node.parentElement){var same=Array.from(node.parentElement.children).filter(function(x){return x.tagName===node.tagName});if(same.length>1)part+=':nth-of-type('+(same.indexOf(node)+1)+')'}
    parts.unshift(part);var candidate=parts.join(' > ');try{if(document.querySelectorAll(candidate).length===1)return candidate}catch(_){}
    node=node.parentElement;
  }
  return parts.join(' > ')
}

var pickState=null;
function startPick(index){
  cancelPick();modal().classList.remove('open');
  var hl=document.createElement('div'),msg=document.createElement('div');
  hl.className='ppz-wl-pickhl';msg.className='ppz-wl-pickmsg';msg.textContent='Tap an item on the page to target this tutorial step · Esc to cancel';
  document.body.appendChild(hl);document.body.appendChild(msg);pickState={index:index,hl:hl,msg:msg};
  document.addEventListener('mousemove',pickMove,true);document.addEventListener('click',pickClick,true);document.addEventListener('keydown',pickKey,true);
}
function pickMove(e){if(!pickState)return;var el=e.target;if(!el||el===document.body||el.closest('#ppz-wl-modal,#ppz-wl-tour,#ppz-wl-popwrap,.ppz-wl-pickmsg')){pickState.hl.style.display='none';return}
  var r=el.getBoundingClientRect(),h=pickState.hl;h.style.display='block';h.style.left=r.left+'px';h.style.top=r.top+'px';h.style.width=r.width+'px';h.style.height=r.height+'px'}
function pickClick(e){
  if(!pickState)return;var el=e.target;
  if(!el||el===document.body||el.closest('#ppz-wl-modal,#ppz-wl-tour,#ppz-wl-popwrap,.ppz-wl-pickmsg'))return;
  e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();if(e.stopImmediatePropagation)e.stopImmediatePropagation();
  var css=selectorFor(el),idx=pickState.index;cancelPick();if(work&&work.tour.steps[idx])work.tour.steps[idx].target=css;
  modal().classList.add('open');tab='tour';draw();
}
function pickKey(e){if(e.key==='Escape'){e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();cancelPick();modal().classList.add('open');draw()}}
function cancelPick(){if(!pickState)return;pickState.hl.remove();pickState.msg.remove();pickState=null;
  document.removeEventListener('mousemove',pickMove,true);document.removeEventListener('click',pickClick,true);document.removeEventListener('keydown',pickKey,true)}

function patchOut(html){
  if(typeof html!=='string')return html;
  var tag='<script type="application/json" id="'+ID+'">'+json()+'<\/script>';
  var re=/<script type="application\/json" id="ppz-welcome-data">[\s\S]*?<\/script>/i;
  if(re.test(html))return html.replace(re,tag);
  var k=html.toLowerCase().lastIndexOf('</body>');return k>=0?html.slice(0,k)+tag+'\n'+html.slice(k):html+tag;
}
function wrapFns(){['buildHTML','cleanHTML'].forEach(function(n){var f=window[n];if(typeof f==='function'&&!f.__ppzWl){var w=function(){var r=f.apply(this,arguments);return r&&typeof r.then==='function'?r.then(patchOut):patchOut(r)};w.__ppzWl=true;window[n]=w}})}

function autoStart(){
  renderBanners();
  if(state.tour.on&&state.tour.steps.length&&String(ls(SEEN,'')||'')!==String(state.tour.ver))startTour();
  else showPopups();
}
function init(){
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-ppz-wl-ui]').forEach(function(n){n.remove()});
  load();renderBanners();wrapFns();ensureOwner();ensureSettings();
  setTimeout(autoStart,900);
  var tick=function(){ensureOwner();ensureSettings();wrapFns()};
  setInterval(tick,1500);
  if(document.body&&window.MutationObserver){var ob=new MutationObserver(function(){tick()});ob.observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true})}
  window.PPZWelcome={data:function(){return state},start:function(){startTour()},announcements:function(){showPopups(true)},save:function(){save()}};
}
if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',init);else init();
})();

function safeLinkify(el,text){
 if(!CFG.clickableLinks){
  const mention='@'+name;const re=new RegExp('('+mention.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g,'\\$&')+')','ig');
  text.split(re).forEach(part=>{if(part.toLowerCase()===mention.toLowerCase()){const mark=document.createElement('mark');mark.textContent=part;mark.style.background='#5b438d';mark.style.color='#fff';mark.style.borderRadius='3px';el.append(mark)}else el.append(document.createTextNode(part))});return;

 while((m=re.exec(text))){el.append(document.createTextNode(text.slice(at,m.index)));try{const u=new URL(m[0]);if(u.protocol==='http:'||u.protocol==='https:'){const a=document.createElement('a');a.href=u.href;a.textContent=m[0];a.target='_blank';a.rel='noopener noreferrer';el.append(a)}else el.append(document.createTextNode(m[0]))}catch(_){el.append(document.createTextNode(m[0]))}at=m.index+m[0].length}
 el.append(document.createTextNode(text.slice(at)));
}

 rows.forEach(m=>{
  const wrap=document.createElement('article');wrap.className='msg';
  const meta=document.createElement('div');meta.className='meta';const who=document.createElement('b');who.textContent=m.uid===uid?'You':(m.name||'Guest');who.style.color=/^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(m.color||'')?m.color:'#a78bfa';meta.append(who);if(m.role==='admin'||m.acct){const tg=document.createElement('span');tg.className='tag';tg.textContent=m.role==='admin'?'👑 admin':'✓ member';meta.append(tg)}
  const when=document.createElement('span');when.textContent=m.ts?new Date(m.ts).toLocaleTimeString([], {hour:'numeric',minute:'2-digit'}):'';meta.append(when);wrap.append(meta);
  if(m.reply&&m.reply.n){const rp=document.createElement('div');rp.className='reply';rp.textContent='Replying to '+m.reply.n+': '+(m.reply.t||'');wrap.append(rp)}
  const body=document.createElement('div');body.className='body';safeLinkify(body,escText(m.text));wrap.append(body);
  const tools=document.createElement('div');tools.className='meta';const reply=document.createElement('button');reply.className='ctl';reply.dataset.chat='reply';reply.dataset.id=m.id;reply.textContent='Reply';tools.append(reply);
  if(m.uid===uid||isAdmin){const del=document.createElement('button');del.className='ctl';del.dataset.chat='delete';del.dataset.id=m.id;del.textContent='Delete';tools.append(del)}
  if(isAdmin&&m.uid!==uid){const ban=document.createElement('button');ban.className='ctl';ban.dataset.chat='ban';ban.dataset.uid=m.uid;ban.textContent='Ban user';tools.append(ban)}
  wrap.append(tools);messagesEl.append(wrap);
 });
 messagesEl.scrollTop=messagesEl.scrollHeight;
}
function drawAdmin(){adminBtn.hidden=!isAdmin;adminLoginBtn.hidden=isAdmin;adminBox.replaceChildren();if(!isAdmin)return;
 const make=(label,action)=>{const b=document.createElement('button');b.className='ctl';b.dataset.chat=action;b.textContent=label;adminBox.append(b)};
 make('Live announcement','live');make('Slow mode','slow');make('Clear chat','clear');
}
function drawAccount(){loginBtn.hidden=isMember;logoutBtn.hidden=!isMember}
function friendly(err){const c=String(err&&err.code||'');
 if(c==='auth/email-already-in-use')return 'That username is already taken.';
 if(/invalid-credential|wrong-password|user-not-found|invalid-login/.test(c))return 'Wrong username or password.';
 if(c==='auth/operation-not-allowed')return 'Email/Password sign-in is not turned on yet in Firebase (Authentication > Sign-in method).';
 if(c==='auth/weak-password')return 'Choose a longer password (6+ characters).';
 if(c==='auth/too-many-requests')return 'Too many tries. Wait a minute and try again.';
 if(c==='auth/network-request-failed')return 'Network problem. Check your connection.';
 return err&&err.message?err.message:'Something went wrong.'}
authBox.addEventListener('click',async e=>{
 const b=e.target.closest('[data-m]');if(!b)return;const m=b.dataset.m;
 if(m==='x'){authBox.hidden=true;return}
 if(!A||!auth){authMsg.textContent='Chat is not connected yet.';return}
 const u=authForm.u.value.trim(),p=authForm.p.value;
 if(!/^[A-Za-z0-9_]{3,20}$/.test(u)){authMsg.textContent='Username: 3-20 letters, numbers or _ only.';return}
 if(p.length<6){authMsg.textContent='Password must be at least 6 characters.';return}
 const email=u.toLowerCase()+'@proxypathz.chat';authMsg.textContent='Working…';
 try{
  if(m==='up'){const cred=await A.createUserWithEmailAndPassword(auth,email,p);try{await A.updateProfile(cred.user,{displayName:u})}catch(_){}name=u;drawAccount();say('Account created. Connected as '+name+' (account)')}
  else await A.signInWithEmailAndPassword(auth,email,p);
  authForm.p.value='';authBox.hidden=true;
 }catch(err){authMsg.textContent=friendly(err)}
});
authForm.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Enter'){e.preventDefault();authBox.querySelector('[data-m="in"]').click()}});
async function start(user){
 unsubs.forEach(f=>{try{f()}catch(_){}});unsubs=[];
 uid=user.uid;isMember=!user.isAnonymous;isAdmin=false;isBanned=false;
 const stored=localStorage.getItem('ppz_chat_name');
 name=isMember?(user.displayName||String(user.email||'').split('@')[0]||'Member'):((stored&&stored.trim().length>=2&&stored.trim().length<=20)?stored.trim():'Guest');
 known=new Set();rows=[];firstMessages=true;render();drawAccount();input.disabled=false;form.querySelector('button').disabled=false;
 try{isAdmin=(await get(ref(db,'admins/'+uid))).val()===true}catch(_){isAdmin=false}
 if(!isAdmin){try{await set(ref(db,'adminProbe/'+uid),true);isAdmin=true}catch(_){}}   // works if this account already unlocked admin with the admin password
 drawAdmin();render();
 const label=()=>'Connected as '+name+(isMember?' (account)':' (guest)');
 unsubs.push(onValue(ref(db,'bans/'+uid),s=>{isBanned=!!s.val();input.disabled=isBanned;form.querySelector('button').disabled=isBanned;say(isBanned?'You cannot post in this chat.':label())}));
 try{await set(ref(db,'presence/'+uid),true);onDisconnect(ref(db,'presence/'+uid)).remove()}catch(_){}
 unsubs.push(onValue(ref(db,'settings/slowMs'),s=>{const v=Number(s.val());if(Number.isFinite(v)&&v>=1000&&v<=600000)slowMs=v}));
 unsubs.push(onValue(ref(db,'settings/live'),s=>{const v=s.val();liveEl.textContent=v&&v.text?String(v.text):'';liveEl.style.display=v&&v.text?'block':'none'}));
 unsubs.push(onValue(query(ref(db,'messages'),orderByChild('ts'),limitToLast(CFG.history)),snap=>{
   const v=snap.val()||{},next=Object.entries(v).map(([id,m])=>({...m,id})).sort((a,b)=>(a.ts||0)-(b.ts||0));
   if(!firstMessages&&!isOpen){let inc=0;for(const m of next)if(!known.has(m.id)&&m.uid!==uid)inc++;if(inc)setUnread(unread+inc)}
   known=new Set(next.map(m=>m.id));rows=next;firstMessages=false;render();
 }));
 say(label());
}
function configReady(){return Object.values(FIREBASE_CONFIG).every(v=>v&&v!=='PASTE_HERE')}
async function boot(){
 if(!configReady()){say('Firebase setup needed: fill in FIREBASE_CONFIG in this add-on.');input.disabled=true;form.querySelector('button').disabled=true;return}
 try{
  // try Google's server first; if it is blocked on this network, try jsDelivr instead
  const SOURCES=[
   ['https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/10.12.2/firebase-app.js','https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/10.12.2/firebase-auth.js','https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/10.12.2/firebase-database.js'],
   ['https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/firebase@10.12.2/app/+esm','https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/firebase@10.12.2/auth/+esm','https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/firebase@10.12.2/database/+esm']
  ];
  let appMod=null,authMod=null,dbMod=null,loadErr=null;
  for(const [a,b,c] of SOURCES){try{appMod=await import(a);authMod=await import(b);dbMod=await import(c);loadErr=null;break}catch(e){loadErr=e;appMod=authMod=dbMod=null}}
  if(!appMod)throw loadErr||new Error('Could not load Firebase');
  const app=appMod.initializeApp(FIREBASE_CONFIG,'ppz-chat');auth=authMod.getAuth(app);db=dbMod.getDatabase(app);
  A=authMod;({ref,push,update,set,remove,get,onValue,onDisconnect,serverTimestamp,query,orderByChild,limitToLast}=dbMod);
  // guests get an anonymous session; accounts keep their login between visits
  A.onAuthStateChanged(auth,async user=>{
    if(!user){try{await A.signInAnonymously(auth)}catch(err){say('Could not connect: '+friendly(err))}return}
    await start(user);
  });
 }catch(err){const m=err&&err.message?err.message:'unknown error';say('Chat connection failed: '+m+(/dynamically imported|Failed to fetch/i.test(m)?' (The Firebase scripts were blocked. Open the live site in a normal browser tab, or try another network.)':''));input.disabled=true;form.querySelector('button').disabled=true}
}
form.addEventListener('submit',async e=>{
 e.preventDefault();if(!db||!uid||isBanned)return;const raw=input.value.trim();if(!raw)return;if(raw.length>CFG.maxLen){say('Message is too long.');return}
 const wait=slowMs-(Date.now()-lastSent);if(wait>0){say('Please wait '+Math.ceil(wait/1000)+'s before sending again.');return}
 const text=filterWords(raw);const key=push(ref(db,'messages')).key;const m={uid,name,text,ts:serverTimestamp(),color:userColor};if(isMember)m.acct=true;if(isAdmin)m.role='admin';if(replyTo)m.reply={n:replyTo.n,t:replyTo.t};
 try{await update(ref(db),{['messages/'+key]:m,['lastPost/'+uid]:serverTimestamp()});lastSent=Date.now();input.value='';replyTo=null;input.placeholder='Write a message…';say('Message sent.')}catch(err){say('Message not sent: '+err.message)}
});
boot();
})();
</script>
